Abstract: Autonomous drone racing is a fundamentally challenging regime for autonomous aerial robots, requiring time-optimal control while operating under persistent actuation saturation. While reinforcement learning (RL) has achieved human-level performance in this domain, current methods fail to generalize; policies trained on specific environments often crash immediately in unseen configurations. This failure reflects the intrinsic difficulty of zero-shot generalization in agile flight, arising from high-dimensional task variation and the tight coupling between safety and performance at high speeds. Existing approaches that improve generalization impose a substantial cost on flight speed: control policies must significantly degrade performance to achieve even modest levels of generalization. In this work, we propose a framework for zero-shot generalization in agile flight for RL-based drone racing. By combining task-aware switching based on learning progress with a physically informed procedural track generator, the framework produces a fast and robust generalist policy without test-time adaptation. Our method achieves strong zero-shot performance across a wide range of unseen racetracks in the real world, demonstrating a 7.4x improvement in generalization over the state-of-the-art approaches, while maintaining competitive racing speeds. We validate our method's results in both simulation and real-world settings, including a challenging vision-based, end-to-end control setting that operates without explicit state estimation, where all prior approaches fail to generalize.

</details>


### [215] [Continual Robot Policy Learning via Variational Neural Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27353)
*Jiaxu Xing,Zhiyuan Zhu,Yunfan Ren,Ismail Geles,Yifan Zhai,Rudolf Reiter,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种机器人持续学习框架，通过深度学习与物理先验结合的动态模型进行实时环境识别，实现了对非平稳环境下机器人策略的高效快速适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统机器人学习控制器在部署后无法适应复杂、动态变动（如风力、负载变化等）环境的问题，克服静态策略难以应对环境非平稳性的缺陷。

Method: 1. 构建条件感知动力学模型：结合物理分析先验与残差神经网络；2. 引入循环编码器（Recurrent Encoder）：从实时交互中推理隐藏的环境动态参数；3. 基于可微仿真（Differentiable Simulation）的策略学习：利用潜在空间采样进行策略训练；4. 在线识别：部署时通过实时推理替代采样，实现对重复及未知环境动态的快速响应。

Result: 在四旋翼轨迹跟踪实验中，该方法能在约1秒内自动适应重复性干扰（比在线残差重拟合快5倍），并将大干扰下的悬停和跟踪误差分别显著降低了65.7%和53.3%。

Conclusion: 该框架通过在线环境识别代替传统的实时重拟合，成功实现了在动态多变环境中的快速自适应，证明了利用持续学习和隐式状态估计可显著提升机器人政策的稳健性与适应能力。

Abstract: Robots deployed in the real world rarely operate under a single fixed dynamics model: wind changes, payloads vary, batteries drain, contacts shift, and hardware wears. Yet most learning-based controllers are trained once and deployed as if learning were complete. This prevents the robot from using deployment experience to further improve task performance. In this work, we propose a continual learning framework that uses real-world experience to improve robot policies under hidden and recurring dynamics. Our method learns a condition-aware dynamics model from real state-action trajectories by combining an analytical physics prior with a neural residual for unmodeled effects. A recurrent encoder infers the current hidden condition from recent interaction, and this estimate conditions both the residual model and the policy. Policy learning is performed via differentiable simulation using diverse learned dynamics sampled from the latent model. At deployment, these sampled conditions are replaced by conditions inferred online from recent real interaction, allowing the policy to recover recurring dynamics by recognition rather than residual re-fitting. Through extensive simulation studies and real-world experiments, we demonstrate that the framework improves policy performance under diverse unobserved disturbances. On real quadrotor trajectory tracking under changing wind, the policy recovers from recurring disturbances in roughly 1s, about 5x faster than online residual re-fitting. It also reduces large-disturbance hover and tracking errors by 65.7% and 53.3% over the state-of-the-art online adaptation approaches

</details>


### [216] [RouterVLA: Turning Smoke Tests into Supervision for Heterogeneous VLA Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27355)
*Xingyu Ren,Chugang Yi,Ge Ma,Youran Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于预部署评估数据重用的机器人策略路由（RouterVLA）方法，通过严格的数据分离确保评估可靠性，显著提升了机器人系统的整体成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人团队通常会对候选VLA策略进行预部署测试（smoke testing），但这些宝贵的评估数据在选定策略后往往被丢弃。本研究旨在探讨如何复用这些预部署评估记录，以实现更优的策略路由（policy selection）。

Method: 提出了RouterVLA框架，采用“基于结果不相交的交叉拟合”技术：利用已有的预部署评估记录（probes/profiles）为每个VLA专家模型构建性能档案，并使用独立的试验来评估被选中的专家，避免数据交叉污染。

Result: 在34,752条LIBERO-Plus记录上测试，采用该方法将测试集成功率从0.4686提升至0.6149（提升14.64个百分点）。研究还发现，如果不进行结果分离，性能评估会产生1.87倍的虚高。

Conclusion: 通过将预部署评估（commissioning）与策略路由（routing）相结合，利用“基于结果不相交的交叉拟合”（outcome-disjoint cross-fitting）方法，在无需额外计算开销的情况下，可显著提升机器人团队的整体成功率。该研究强调，在模型评估中保持数据分离以避免性能评估膨胀至关重要。

Abstract: We study whether pre-deployment evaluation rollouts can be reused to supervise policy selection. Robot teams routinely smoke test candidate vision-language-action (VLA) policies, then compress those trials into a global winner. RouterVLA evaluates this idea with outcome-disjoint cross-fitting: recorded probes build a profile for each frozen expert, and a separate trial scores the selected expert without entering its profile. Across 34,752 LIBERO-Plus rollout records, a transparent probe-success rule raises held-out success from 0.4686 to 0.6149, a +14.64pp gain. Under the scalar-only profiles studied here, learned scorers are statistically indistinguishable from this rule, showing that commissioning carries the routing value while extra scalar scorer capacity does not create it. Reusing the scored trial inflates the measured gain by $1.87\times$, so credible ledger routing needs outcome separation; model scaling improves individual policies, while commissioning-aware routing improves the system built from them.

</details>


### [217] [World Action Models Enable Continual Imitation Learning with Recurrent Generative Replays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27374)
*Manish Kumar Govind,Dominick Reilly,Smit Patel,Hieu Le,Srijan Das*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REGEN利用世界行动模型（WAMs）生成伪回放轨迹，使机器人能够在无需存储原始演示数据的情况下进行持续模仿学习，有效缓解了灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人持续模仿学习中，如何避免灾难性遗忘而不依赖存储原始人类演示数据（受限于隐私和内存）是一个关键挑战。利用生成式模型合成数据进行回放成为一种潜在解决方案。

Method: 提出了循环生成式回放（REGEN）框架。利用已训练的WAMs，在持续学习过程中递归地合成与先前任务指令和当前任务观测相一致的伪回放轨迹，无需访问原始人类演示数据。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人操作实验中，REGEN较序列微调（sequential fine-tuning）将灾难性遗忘减少了多达50%，且性能接近于需要访问真实历史数据的特权经验回放方法。

Conclusion: REGEN验证了世界行动模型（WAMs）在实现无存储演示的持续机器人学习方面的潜力。实验表明，该方法能有效减轻灾难性遗忘，同时揭示了长程视觉退化和动作-观测不一致性是当前生成式回放的核心瓶颈。

Abstract: Going beyond predicting robot actions, World Action Models (WAMs) can also generate future visual observations. We build on this generative capability to propose Recurrent Generative Replay (REGEN), a continual imitation learning framework that synthesizes pseudo-replay trajectories, enabling a robot policy to rehearse previously learned tasks without storing their original human demonstrations. During continual adaptation, REGEN recursively queries the WAM to synthesize pseudo-replay trajectories conditioned only on prior task instructions and current-task observations. Experiments in both simulation and real-world manipulation settings show that REGEN reduces catastrophic forgetting by up to $50\%$ relative to sequential fine-tuning, while approaching the performance of privileged experience replay methods that require access to real replay data. Finally, we analyze the factors limiting generated replay, identifying long-horizon visual degradation and action-observation inconsistency as the primary bottlenecks. Our results establish WAMs as a promising foundation for continual robot learning without stored demonstrations.

</details>


### [218] [Scalable Behavior Cloning with Open Data, Training, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27375)
*Arthur Allshire,Himanshu Gaurav Singh,Ritvik Singh,Adam Rashid,Hongsuk Choi,David McAllister,Justin Yu,Yiyuan Chen,Huang Huang,Pieter Abbeel,Xi Chen,Rocky Duan,Phillip Isola,Jitendra Malik,Fred Shentu,Guanya Shi,Philipp Wu,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ABC是一个全面的机器人行为克隆开源工具包，发布了迄今最大的开源遥操作数据集（ABC-130K），并提供了完整的软硬及仿真生态，助力机器人模仿学习研究的标准化与可复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作研究领域缺乏大规模、统一的开放数据集和可复现的基础设施，导致研究进度缓慢且各实验室间结果难以对比。

Method: 1. 创建并发布了规模达13万人机交互（teleoperation）片段（3500小时）的数据集 ABC-130K。
2. 构建并开源了易于复制的硬件方案、训练基础设施及仿真管线。
3. 提供了400小时的仿真数据及协同训练方案（Co-training recipe）。
4. 对比研究了 Diffusion Transformers (DiT) 和 Vision-Language-Action (VLA) 模型的架构选择与训练策略。

Result: 所训练的策略成功执行了盒折叠和从钱包中取出信用卡等复杂的灵巧操作任务，并证实了仿真测试可以作为真实世界评估的有效代理指标。

Conclusion: ABC 旨在通过开源数据集、硬件和基础设施，降低机器人操作研究的门槛，为行为克隆领域提供一个标准化的研究基准，以提升该领域的模型性能与可复现性。

Abstract: We introduce ABC, a fully open-source stack for manipulation with behavior cloning. At its core is ABC-130K: the largest open-source teleoperation dataset to date, featuring 3,500 hours of data spanning over 130K episodes across 195 diverse tasks. Furthermore, we open-source our accessible hardware setup, training infrastructure, and simulation pipeline. We also release 400 hours of sim-teleop data and provide a co-training recipe that produces correlated simulation and real-world evaluation, offering a reliable proxy for ablating model-design and training decisions before costly real-world evaluation. We explore various training recipes and compare common architectural choices for Diffusion Transformers (DiT) and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, grounding our findings in real-world evaluations. The resulting policies successfully execute dexterous tasks such as box folding and extracting credit cards from wallets. By providing a reproducible toolkit, we aim to place researchers on an equal footing, establishing the necessary foundation to learn the ABCs of Behavior Cloning together as a community.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [219] [Query Cost Model Calibration in Confidential Virtual Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26385)
*Qihan Zhang,Mengyuan Li,Ibrahim Sabek*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对机密虚拟机（CVM）中数据库查询性能下降问题，本文提出了一种轻量级代价模型校准技术，通过优化对数据移动与RMP开销的评估，成功提升了查询执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机密虚拟机（CVM）中运行数据库时，现有的查询优化器仍基于非加密VM（KVM）的假设进行代价评估，导致硬件-软件匹配失调，进而引发分析型查询的显著性能损失。

Method: 提出了一种轻量级的CVM感知代价校准方法（CVM-aware cost calibration）。该方法基于查询优化器现有的物理代理（physical proxies），对数据移动（data movement）和RMP相关转换（RMP-related translation）这两项主要开销进行建模。

Result: 实验表明，该校准方法显著缩小了KVM与CVM之间的性能差距，在部分工作负载上性能最高提升了48%，甚至在某些场景下超越了传统KVM基线的性能。

Conclusion: 机密计算环境下，通过针对CVM特性进行轻量级的代价模型校准，可以显著消除处理器代价评估与实际硬件性能之间的偏差，从而提升数据库查询性能。

Abstract: With the growing adoption of Confidential Computing, running databases in confidential virtual machines (CVMs) such as AMD SEV-SNP has become an attractive way to protect sensitive cloud data with minimal changes to legacy DBMSs. However, analytical queries in such CVMs often suffer substantial overhead, and prior database work has largely stopped at benchmarking these slowdowns rather than optimizing them. We show that this problem stems from a hardware-software mismatch: query optimizers still rely on KVM-oriented (non-encrypted VM) cost assumptions that no longer hold in CVMs. To address this, we propose a lightweight CVM-aware cost calibration. It models two dominant sources of optimizer-facing overhead: data movement and RMP-related translation using simple physical proxies already available to the optimizer. Experiments show that the calibration significantly narrows the KVM/CVM performance gap, recovering up to 48 percent performance and even outperforming the KVM baseline on some workloads.

</details>


### [220] [3D Spatial Pattern Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26465)
*Nicole R. Schneider,Avik Das,Lukas Arzoumanidis,Abhijeet Ghodgaonkar,Hanan Samet,Youness Dehbi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文将空间模式匹配扩展至三维空间，解决了传统二维方法无法处理实体高度信息的局限，并提供了三维子图匹配算法及相关基准测试数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间模式匹配方法大多局限于二维笛卡尔平面，忽略了现实中实体具有高度信息的客观事实，导致在处理如建筑等三维空间数据时存在局限性。

Method: 1. 定义了三维空间模式匹配问题的推广形式；2. 开发了一种能够处理距离关系的三维子图匹配算法；3. 构建并发布了两个三维空间模式匹配数据集（一个合成数据集，一个基于德国汉堡真实建筑数据的数据集）。

Result: 研究成功建立了三维空间模式匹配的定义，验证了所提出的子图匹配算法在合成数据和真实三维城市建筑数据上的可行性，并提供了对比所需的基准结果。

Conclusion: 本文将空间模式匹配从二维拓展至三维，定义了三维空间模式匹配问题，并提出了相应的子图匹配算法及基准数据集，为处理具有高度信息的现实世界实体匹配提供了基础框架。

Abstract: Spatial pattern matching is the process of matching query entities and constraints with database entities and relations. It has many applications, including similar region search, housing market search, landmark search, and road network matching. To our knowledge, all existing spatial pattern matching approaches frame the problem in a 2 dimensional space, where entities lie in a cartesian plane and relationships defined between them are contained in 2 dimensions. However, this problem framing has significant limitations when searching for real world entities that have height in addition to position. To address this limitation, we extend spatial pattern matching to 3 dimensions and provide a generalized definition of the problem. We describe a subgraph matching algorithm capable of resolving 3D spatial patterns over distance relations and release two 3D spatial pattern matching datasets, one synthetic and one containing real 3D building data from the city of Hamburg, Germany. We test our subgraph matching algorithm on both datasets and present results as a baseline for future methods to build upon.

</details>


### [221] [EcoTable: Cost-effective Table Integration in Data Lakes for Natural Language Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26613)
*Yuhui Wang,Jinqi Liu,Chengliang Chai,Hangyu Zhao,Yuhao Deng,Yuyu Luo,Xin Tang,Ye Yuan,Guoren Wang,Fengjin Wang,Lei Cao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: EcoTable 是一种利用 LLM 和图搜索算法的自动化数据集成框架，能够根据自然语言查询自动集成数据湖中的表，显著提升了集成的灵活性、准确性并降低了运算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 ETL 流程依赖人工预定义模式，难以应对数据湖中多样化的数据格式，且难以满足不可预见的分析查询需求，同时现有的人工或自动化集成方法面临成本高、扩展性差的问题。

Method: 1. 构建表关系图：利用轻量级深度学习模型评估表间连接概率；
2. 表识别层：通过两阶段 schema 链接技术定位相关表；
3. 图基验证层：将连接路径计算建模为 Steiner 树搜索，自动发现连接路径和转换需求；
4. 表转换层：利用 LLM 生成实现数据集成和转换的具体执行代码。

Result: 在 4 个真实数据集和超过 200 个查询实验中，EcoTable 的准确率比现有基准提升了 30% 以上，并将 LLM 调用成本降低了 5 倍。

Conclusion: EcoTable 是首个基于自然语言的数据集成框架，通过创新的图搜索和语义理解机制，成功解决了传统 ETL 在灵活性、开发成本和计算效率方面的局限性。

Abstract: The diverse formats of CSV and Parquet files in data lakes pose a significant challenge to traditional ETL, which relies on data engineers to pre-define a target database schema and build a complex pipeline for data integration. Moreover, with this approach, the integrated data often cannot support various analytical needs, as the predefined schema does not necessarily satisfy the table format or join relationships required to answer unforeseen queries. To address this, we propose EcoTable, the first natural language-based data integration framework. Given a set of user-specified natural language queries, EcoTable automatically integrates the tables into a form that adequately supports the corresponding SQL queries. EcoTable achieves this by leveraging the semantic understanding and complex reasoning capabilities of LLMs. Moreover, EcoTable addresses the scalability and cost issues introduced by expensive LLM inferences with a set of novel ideas. First, EcoTable introduces a graph to represent the overall search space, where nodes represent tables and edges carry weights indicating join likelihood produced by a lightweight deep learning model. On top of this graph data structure, EcoTable designs three components to achieve our goal: (1) the table identification layer aims to identify relevant tables via a two-stage schema linking based on user queries; (2) the graph-based validation layer aims to discover significant join paths, including necessary data transformations and bridging tables, by modeling the problem as Steiner tree searches; and (3) the table transformation layer generates transformation code to implement the joins using LLMs. We construct 4 real-world benchmark datasets with more than 200 queries. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EcoTable outperforms the state-of-the-art baselines, increasing accuracy by more than 30% and cutting LLM invocation costs by 5 times.

</details>


### [222] [BtrLog: Low-Latency Logging for Cloud Database Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27051)
*Maximilian Kuschewski,Lam-Duy Nguyen,Matthias Jasny,Tobias Ziegler,Viktor Leis,Muhammad El-Hindi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: BtrLog 是一种高性能、低成本的可复用云日志服务，通过 SSD 节点集群提供低延迟同步写入，并通过异步分段归档至对象存储来降低长期存储成本，解决了云原生数据库日志存储的痛点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的云数据库日志方案存在局限性：远程块存储（如 EBS）延迟高且昂贵，对象存储高延迟且单次追加成本高，而专有的日志后端往往与引擎高度耦合，难以在不同数据库系统中复用。因此，需要一种高性能、低成本且可复用的云日志方案。

Method: 提出名为 BtrLog 的重用式云日志服务，采用“SSD 节点集群日志同步+异步对象存储归档”的架构：1. 日志记录在 SSD 节点集群中进行 quorum（法定人数）复制，通过单次网络往返（RTT）实现持久化；2. 使用异步机制将日志记录大规模分段归档至对象存储，避开关键写路径。

Result: 实验评估显示，BtrLog 在存储延迟方面优于 EBS，并显著提高了集成 DBMS 的端到端事务吞吐量。

Conclusion: BtrLog 通过解耦日志持久化与存储，作为一种通用的云原生日志服务，成功解决了云数据库在延迟、成本与持久性之间的取舍问题，证明了高性能低成本日志服务在现代云原生架构中的可行性。

Abstract: Cloud database systems cannot rely on instance-local disks for write-ahead logging (WAL) durability, forcing WAL onto remote storage. Existing options are unsatisfying: remote block storage like EBS is easy to adopt but adds substantial write latency and cost, while object storage offers excellent durability and low storage cost but is impractical for OLTP due to high latency and per-append cost. Many cloud-native databases, therefore, depend on purpose-built logging backends, which are typically proprietary and tightly coupled to engine-specific replication and recovery protocols, limiting reuse. We present BtrLog, a reusable cloud logging service that combines low-latency durable appends with low-cost archival for the common single-writer architecture. BtrLog replicates log records across a quorum of SSD-backed log nodes in a single network round trip, reducing sensitivity to stragglers in commit latency. To minimize storage cost, log nodes archive records to object storage as large segments, which are written asynchronously and off the latency-critical write path. In our evaluation, BtrLog achieves lower latency than EBS and enables higher end-to-end transaction throughput when integrated into a DBMS.

</details>


### [223] [Understanding Domain-Aware Distribution Alignment in Budgeted Entity Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27342)
*Nicholas Pulsone,Gregory Goren,Roee Shraga*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文深入研究了低资源领域感知实体匹配方法BEACON，通过实验揭示了数据约束、算法选择及分布对齐技术对该框架性能的具体影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管目前的领域感知和低资源实体匹配（EM）方法表现优异，但尚不清楚这些方法在不同的实际数据约束和监督水平下如何运作，缺乏对现有前沿方法BEACON行为的深入理解。

Method: 采用实证研究方法，针对BEACON框架进行了一系列有针对性的对比实验，通过改变算法的选择路径及数据可用性条件，分析其性能表现。

Result: 实验结果明确了BEACON在不同数据限制条件下的表现规律，指出了不同算法配置对最终匹配精度的影响，并量化了分布对齐技术在提升模型鲁棒性方面的贡献。

Conclusion: 通过对BEACON的系统性实验，揭示了算法组件与数据约束对实体匹配性能的具体影响，强调了分布对齐（distribution alignment）在低资源、领域感知场景下的关键作用，为解决实际数据集成中的挑战提供了指导建议。

Abstract: Entity Matching (EM) is a core operation in the data integration pipeline, where records from different sources are compared to determine whether they refer to the same real-world entity. Recent work has incorporated domain information and low-resource learning techniques to better adapt EM systems to realistic settings. While these approaches have demonstrated strong performance, it remains unclear how they behave under varying data constraints and levels of supervision in practice. In this paper, we investigate a state-of-the-art method for low-resource, domain-aware EM--BEACON--and study how its performance is affected by different algorithmic choices and data availability conditions. We conduct a series of targeted experiments to evaluate these variations, providing deeper insight into the role of distribution alignment and the behavior of the BEACON framework.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [224] [The Role of Input Dimensionality in the Emergence and Targeted Control of Adversarial Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26207)
*Nasrin Malekzadeh Goradel,Niccolo Pancino,Yaser Gholizade Atani,Benedetta Tondi,Giovanni Bellettini,Mauro Barni*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本研究系统探讨了输入维度如何影响深度学习的对抗脆弱性，证实了维度增长使对抗样本更容易生成，且定向攻击与非定向攻击之间的难度差异因高维几何效应而减小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于深度神经网络对抗脆弱性的高维几何理论缺乏系统的实证检验，亟需深入研究输入维度在对抗样本生成与定向控制中的真实作用。

Method: 1. 对基于测度集中（Concentration of measure）的现有理论框架进行分析。2. 在不同维度和多样化架构的分层图像数据集上进行大规模实证评估。3. 从理论和实验两个维度对比非定向攻击与定向攻击在不同维度下的表现差异。

Result: 1. 真实图像类别表现出比现有理论假设更强的经验局部化（Empirical localization）。2. 随着输入维度增加，生成对抗样本变得更容易。3. 高维几何暗示定向攻击相比非定向攻击所需的额外畸变非常有限，且该差距随维度增加而进一步缩小。

Conclusion: 高输入维度是导致对抗样本产生及定向攻击难度降低的重要基本因素，但该现象的根源（是高维几何与数据分布的关系，还是神经网络结构属性）仍有待进一步探究。

Abstract: Several theoretical works have tried to explain the adversarial vulnerability of deep neural networks through properties of high-dimensional geometry. However, the assumptions underlying these works are rarely examined empirically, and systematic evidence remains limited. In this work, we present a systematic study of the role of input dimensionality in both the emergence and the targeted control of adversarial examples. We first analyse the scope and limitations of existing theoretical frameworks based on concentration of measure, showing that real image classes exhibit strong empirical localization, beyond what such theories typically assume. We then conduct an extensive empirical evaluation across hierarchical image datasets spanning a wide range of input dimensionalities and diverse neural architectures. Our results consistently show that adversarial examples become easier to construct as dimensionality increases. We also investigate how input dimensionality affects the additional difficulty of crafting targeted adversarial examples. In particular, we provide theoretical arguments showing that high-dimensional geometry implies that enforcing a specific target label entails only a limited additional distortion compared to untargeted attacks. We corroborate this insight through extensive experiments, demonstrating that the gap between targeted and untargeted perturbations remains small and further narrows as input dimensionality increases. While, taken together, our findings establish high input dimensionality as a fundamental factor underlying the emergence and targeted control of adversarial examples, whether this phenomenon primarily arises from the interplay between high-dimensional geometry and data distributions or from the architectural properties of deep neural networks remains an open question.

</details>


### [225] [A probabilistic framework for online test-time adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26457)
*Daniel Corrales,David Ríos Insua*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用状态空间模型进行在线测试时自适应（TTA）的概率框架，解决了模型在面对分布偏移时的适应性难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决机器学习模型在面对由于分布偏移（Distributional Shift）导致的训练与测试环境不一致时，在线进行测试时自适应（Test-time Adaptation）的挑战。

Method: 构建了一个状态空间模型（State-Space Modelling），涵盖参数学习、参数随时间演化、先验调整以及预测四个关键维度。

Result: 通过概率建模方式，使模型能够实时感知并适应分布偏移，提高了模型在不同测试环境下的稳健性和准确性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于状态空间建模的概率框架，能够有效解决训练与测试分布不一致的在线测试时自适应问题，提供了统一的参数与模型更新范式。

Abstract: This paper presents a probabilistic framework for online test-time adaptation problems. In them, a model is trained on labeled data but must adapt to unlabeled data at test time under the assumption that training and test distributions potentially differ, that is, there might have been a distributional shift. The framework is based on a state-space modelling architecture from which parameter learning, parameter time evolution, prior tuning, and prediction can be characterized.

</details>


### [226] [XMSE-Aware Adaptive Empirical Bayes Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26975)
*Minghao Chen,Jiale Zheng*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对传统经验贝叶斯估计在模型误设时性能下降的问题，本文提出了一种基于XMSE的自适应混合估计器，通过动态权衡ML与EB收缩，实现了在正则化有效时利用先验，无效时自动回退至ML的稳健性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统经验贝叶斯（EB）估计虽然在首阶渐近风险上匹配极大似然估计（ML），但在核函数与真实参数偏离时（模型误设），其二阶表现可能极差（XMSE分析揭示其表现可能弱于ML）。为此，需寻找一种自适应混合策略以平衡正则化收益与模型健壮性。

Method: 提出了一种基于交叉均方误差（XMSE）的混合估计器，通过插值结合最大似然估计（ML）与经验贝叶斯（EB）收缩。利用有限样本XMSE近似实现插件式估计，并证明了其二阶预言者遗憾界（oracle regret rate）。

Result: 理论上证明了该估计器在预言者遗憾界上的稳健性，仿真实验及Silverbox等公开基准测试表明，该方法在正则化有效时表现优异，在模型误设时能自动退回到ML，展现了良好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于XMSE的混合估计器，它能有效利用正则化带来的收益，并在核函数模型误设时自动向极大似然估计回退，在多种基准测试中表现优于传统方法。

Abstract: Empirical Bayes (EB) estimators can match the first-order asymptotic risk of maximum likelihood (ML) while behaving very differently at second order: recent excess mean squared error (XMSE) analysis shows that kernel-based EB estimation may be worse than ML when the kernel is poorly aligned with the true parameter. This paper turns that diagnostic into a design principle. We propose an XMSE-aware mixed estimator that interpolates between ML and EB shrinkage. Its fixed-weight XMSE is a scalar quadratic, yielding a closed-form oracle mixing weight that is no worse than both ML and the base EB estimator at the XMSE scale. A plug-in implementation based on finite-sample XMSE approximations is proved consistent, with a second-order oracle regret rate for an interior oracle weight. We further establish a transfer of the regret bound to the fixed-weight risk curve evaluated at the selected weight, a thresholded boundary rule, and extensions to compact kernel families and to finite and growing kernel dictionaries with high-probability oracle bounds. Finite impulse response simulations with SURE-tuned, hard-selection, and trace-corrected baselines, together with the public Silverbox and Cascaded Tanks benchmarks, show that the proposed estimator retains most of the benefit of regularization when it is helpful and retreats toward ML under kernel misspecification, with an identified finite-de analyzed on the benchmarks.

</details>


### [227] [Beyond Global Divergences: A Local-Mass Perspective on Bayesian Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27090)
*Hanli Xu,Fengxiang He,Sarat Moka*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对全局度量（如KL散度）无法捕捉局部质量变化的问题，本文提出了“质量指数”和“正则化扩展KL（RE-KL）”两个工具，从理论上量化并阐述了贝叶斯更新过程中局部质量的行为规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球目标函数（如KL散度、ELBO）虽然在贝叶斯推断中被广泛应用，但它们无法直接捕捉分布在局部空间（局部质量）的行为特征。因此，作者旨在建立一套分析局部质量行为的理论框架。

Method: 本文提出了两种数学工具：(1) 质量指数（Mass Index），用于刻画局部质量的幂级和对数级衰减尺度；(2) 正则化扩展KL散度（RE-KL），这是一种能够在存在奇异分量时进行计算的局部化散度。研究者利用这些工具分析了参数支持和似然因子对局部质量演变的影响，并推导了相应的约束不等式。

Result: 研究结果表明：(1) 幂对数似然因子会显式改变局部质量；(2) 参数依赖的支持及其平滑处理会通过影响参数附近的剩余质量来改变局部尺度；(3) 证明了在两种KL方向下比较局部小球质量的绝对、相对及方向性不等式。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入质量指数和正则化扩展KL散度，建立了一套系统的局部质量行为分析框架，证明了KL散度在局部尺度下的不等式性质，揭示了贝叶斯更新如何改变局部质量分布。

Abstract: Global objectives, such as KL divergence and ELBO, are widely used in Bayesian inference for measuring distributional discrepancy. This paper studies their local-mass behaviour that is not directly captured by such objectives. We introduce and use two mathematical tools: (1) Mass Index for recording the polynomial and logarithmic decay scales of local mass, and (2) regularised extended KL (RE-KL), a set-localised divergence that can be formulated in the presence of singular components. Mass Indices help characterise how Bayesian updating changes local mass: (1) power-log likelihood factors shift it explicitly, and (2) parameter-dependent supports, or their smooth softenings, may change the local scale through the amount of mass that remains near the parameter value. Using local RE-KL, we prove absolute, relative, and directional inequalities for comparing local small-ball masses under the two KL directions. Together, these results provide a local theoretical account of local mass behaviour. Experiments provide controlled illustrations of the local behaviour. Code is available at https://github.com/Forsythia0604/Local-Mass-Framework.

</details>


### [228] [Ribbon: Scalable Approximation and Robust Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27269)
*Graham Gibson,John Tipton,Kellin Rumsey,Natalie Klein*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: Ribbon 提出了一种利用影响函数线性化逼近自助法的不确定性量化方法，实现了在无需多次重训练的前提下，高效、鲁棒地估计模型预测不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化复杂、高维或模型误设定下的预测不确定性非常困难。传统的全贝叶斯采样或重采样（如自助法）在现代机器学习中计算成本过高，难以应用。

Method: 使用影响函数（influence function）对单次训练的模型进行局部线性化，以逼近狄利克雷加权自助法（Dirichlet-reweighted bootstrap）的效果。该方法仅需后验的线性代数运算，无需多次重训练。

Result: 在合成回归、MNIST 分类和 California Housing 等数据集上，Ribbon 表现出与全重训练相媲美的预测性能，并能通过验证集调整不确定性尺度以实现更好的预测校准。

Conclusion: Ribbon 是一种鲁棒、计算高效的预测不确定性量化方法。它通过影响函数（influence function）线性化模拟贝叶斯自助法，在避免昂贵的重复训练的同时，提供了具有竞争力的性能和更好的校准效果。

Abstract: Reliably quantifying predictive uncertainty is difficult for complex, high-dimensional, or misspecified models. Both fully Bayesian and bootstrap resampling methods provide principled uncertainty estimates but are often too expensive for modern machine-learning models because they require posterior sampling or repeated model refitting. We introduce Ribbon, a scalable approximation to Dirichlet-reweighted bootstrap uncertainty. Ribbon replaces repeated refitting with an influence-function linearization around a single fitted model, preserving the first-order data-reweighting structure of the Bayesian bootstrap while requiring only post-hoc linear algebra. Ribbon approximates the Bayesian-bootstrap or weighted-likelihood-bootstrap refitting target. With a general concentration parameter, Ribbon gives a calibrated Dirichlet-reweighting family whose uncertainty scale can be tuned on validation data. We show that Ribbon is asymptotically equivalent to a flat-prior Laplace approximation under correct likelihood specification and recovers the robust sandwich covariance under misspecification. Across synthetic regression, MNIST classification, and California Housing benchmarks, Ribbon provides competitive predictive performance and improved calibration in several settings while avoiding repeated model retraining.

</details>


### [229] [When are likely answers right? On Sequence Probability and Correctness in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27359)
*Johannes Zenn,Jonas Geiping*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了序列概率（Sequence Probability）与正确性之间复杂且受限的关系，指出通过优化解码方法来提升序列概率并不总是能转化为更好的模型准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的解码方法普遍基于“通过增加序列概率来提升性能”的假设，但缺乏对序列概率与输出“正确性”之间是否存在必然联系的系统性量化证明。

Method: 通过多维度量化分析序列概率与准确性的关系，包括：1) 不同解码方法间的对比；2) 同一方法内超参数的变化；3) 数据集内不同提示词-答案对的分布；4) 同一提示词下多次采样响应的对比。

Result: 1. 在跨数据集的问题间，高序列概率通常预示较高的正确性；2. 通过调参或切换解码方法强制增加序列概率，并不能稳定提升准确率；3. 针对同一提示词的多个回答，序列概率无法有效区分回答的正确性。

Conclusion: 序列概率（Sequence Probability）与模型准确性之间的关系存在局限性：它在不同问题间能预测准确度，但在特定问题内部或通过超参数调节来提高单个问题的准确度时，并不具备通用性。解码策略应从追求单纯的序列概率最大化转向更精细的评估机制。

Abstract: Many decoding methods for large language models can be understood as shifting probability mass toward outputs that are more likely under the model, either locally at the token level or globally at the sequence level. Therefore, their success depends on a fundamental question: when does sequence probability, that is, the conditional probability of a continuation given a prompt, actually align with correctness? In this paper, we set out to quantify this relationship across decoding methods, models, and benchmarks at four levels: across decoding methods, across hyperparameters within a method, across prompt-answer pairs within a dataset, and across repeated responses to the same prompt. We find that higher sequence probability is often predictive of correctness across prompt-answer pairs within a fixed dataset. However, this relationship does not generally transfer to decoding decisions: increasing sequence probability by changing hyperparameters or methods does not reliably improve accuracy. Further, sequence probability is not a good indicator of correctness for responses to the same prompt. These findings clarify when decoding can and cannot be expected to improve correctness, and provide practical guidance for decoding, self-consistency, and verifier-free self-improvement.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [230] [Reducing Redundancy in Whole-Slide Image Patching for Scalable Indexing and Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26157)
*Jialiang Geng,Ghazal Alabtah,Saghir Alfasly,Wataru Uegami,H. R. Tizhoosh*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 为应对数字病理学中 WSI 大规模存储成本高的痛点，ARReST 通过剔除对分类贡献最小的“对立补丁”来优化索引，在大幅降低存储和计算开销的同时，有效保持了高精度的图像检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全切片图像（WSIs）的快速增长带来存储成本高昂的问题。为支持检索增强生成（RAG）等高风险临床决策 AI，需要一种既能降低存储/计算开销又能保持高检索精度的 WSI 索引方法。

Method: 提出 ARReST (Antithetical Redundancy Reduction Strategy)，一种基于对比冗余减少的框架。该方法不局限于类内去重，而是识别并剔除那些对跨类鉴别贡献最小的“对立补丁”（antithetical patches），从而在压缩索引的同时保留形态多样性和检索保真度。

Result: 在 TCGA 基准测试中，该方法实现了 3% 至 60%（平均 14%±13%）的存储节省，且在大多数器官中未降低检索性能。

Conclusion: ARReST 是一种可扩展且具成本效益的 WSI 索引策略，能够显著减少存储需求并提高检索速度，同时保持临床 AI 系统所需的检索精度，是下一代检索驱动型临床 AI 的理想选择。

Abstract: The rapid growth of digital pathology has created an urgent need for efficient indexing and retrieval of whole slide images (WSIs). This need is intensified by emerging generative AI workflows, particularly retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), which require dependable similarity search to support high-stakes clinical decision-making. Yet the substantial cost of high-performance storage limits the scalability and accessibility of WSI indexing for many healthcare institutions. Consequently, methods that can reduce storage demands while preserving retrieval accuracy have become a critical research priority. We propose ARReST (Antithetical Redundancy Reduction Strategy), a principled oppositional framework that leverages redundancy across dissimilar tissue classes to markedly decrease the number of patches that must be indexed from each WSI. Instead of eliminating only within-class duplicates, ARReST identifies antithetical patches-those whose representations contribute minimally to cross-class discrimination-and prunes them from the searchable archive. This targeted reduction substantially compresses the index without sacrificing morphological diversity or retrieval fidelity. By minimizing superfluous patch representations, ARReST reduces storage footprint, lowers computational overhead, and accelerates similarity search across large pathology repositories. Extensive experiments on TCGA repository (The Cancer Genome Atlas with 21 organs) demonstrate that ARReST achieves significant index compression while maintaining competitive retrieval performance. The observed storage savings of 3% to 60% (14%$\pm$13%) can be reliably achieved without compromising retrieval performance for many organs. The proposed strategy enables scalable, cost-efficient WSI indexing and is well-suited for next-generation retrieval-driven clinical AI systems.

</details>


### [231] [From Clicks to Intent: Cross-Platform Session Embeddings with LLM-Distilled Taxonomy for Financial Services Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26277)
*Dianjing Fan,Yao Li,Kyaw Hpone Myint,Dwipam Katariya,Alexandre G. R. Day,Pranab Mohanty,Giri Iyengar*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对金融领域跨端行为割裂问题，本文提出了一种整合Transformer嵌入（用于提升推荐指标）与LLM知识蒸馏（用于意图可解释性）的双目标框架，有效实现了指标优化与业务洞察的共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融服务中存在跨端链路断层（Pre-login Web与Authenticated App），导致Web侧丰富的意图行为信号在App端个性化推荐中利用不足，且现有方法难以兼顾“推荐指标提升”与“意图可解释性”。

Method: 提出了一个双轨处理架构：1. 使用自监督Transformer将原始Web点击流编码为紧凑的会话嵌入（Session Embedding）；2. 利用基于LLM的分类体系生成与蒸馏流水线，产生可解释的意图标签。

Result: 在生产环境测试中，会话嵌入使移动端主页排序的Recall@1提升1.88%，Log Loss降低13.38%；在转化预测任务中，嵌入表现优于LLM标签（微观F1提升4.3%），且蒸馏层在保持极低延迟的同时，仅损失了7%的性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的双目标意图预测框架（融合自监督嵌入与LLM蒸馏标签）能够有效桥接匿名Web端与实名App端的用户行为，在提升量化推荐精度的同时，提供了大规模、低延迟的定性意图分析能力。

Abstract: Sequential user behavior modeling is widely adopted in industrial recommender systems; however, significant gaps remain in financial services, where pre-login web interactions and authenticated in-app experiences differ drastically. Specifically, pre-login web users typically explore new products, whereas logged-in app users focus on account servicing. Due to the challenge of cross-channel entity resolution (e.g., matching anonymous web sessions to authenticated mobile accounts), web-based intent signals remain underutilized for post-authentication personalization. Existing methods for capturing web-based intent are often ad-hoc and narrow, lacking the flexibility to support both quantitative downstream recommendations and qualitative understanding at scale. In this work, we propose a scalable and dual-purpose intent prediction framework for web-based interactions and demonstrate its applicability for personalization. Our approach transforms raw web clickstreams into two outputs: a self-supervised Transformer encodes multi-modal clickstreams into a compact session embedding, while an LLM-based taxonomy generation and distillation pipeline produces interpretable intent labels. Our system demonstrates that self-supervised clickstream representations combined with LLM-distilled taxonomies can jointly serve quantitative tasks and qualitative understanding in production: on the mobile homepage tile ranking task, the session embedding improves macro Recall@1 by 1.88% and reduces Log Loss by 13.38% over production baselines. On the user conversion prediction task, the embedding outperforms the LLM labels by 4.3% on micro F1, while the distillation layer delivers interpretable labels at ultra-low latency with only a 7% performance drop.

</details>


### [232] [Scoring Is Not Enough: Addressing Gaps in Utility-fairness Trade-offs for Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26369)
*Shubham Singh,Ian A. Kash,Mesrob I. Ohannessian*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究发现，通过训练评分函数来平衡公平性与效用通常是次优的，建议转向使用半贪婪后处理方法以获得更好的性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在信息检索和推荐系统中，传统的基于评分函数（Scoring Functions）的权衡方法在实现公平性与效用平衡时存在局限性，作者旨在揭示并解决这一理论瓶颈。

Method: 通过构建通用公平性框架下的反例进行理论分析，并结合实证实验对比评分函数法与半贪婪后处理的效果。

Result: 理论上证明了在确定性或随机性评分函数及不同公平性衡量尺度下，基于分数的方法均存在次优性；实验表明半贪婪后处理方法能显著优化权衡效果，逼近穷举后处理的理想水平。

Conclusion: 单纯依靠评分函数（Scoring Functions）无法实现所有效用-公平性的最优权衡（Trade-offs）；相比之下，半贪婪后处理（Semi-greedy post-processing）是一种更有效且易于计算的替代方案。

Abstract: Scoring functions are used to represent the relevance of individual documents. In modern information retrieval or recommendation systems, they are often learned from data and play a pivotal role in ranking sets of documents or items in a way that maximizes utility to a query or user. With the recent interest in algorithmic fairness, the success of scoring has naturally led to methods that learn scores that simultaneously trade off fairness and utility. In this work, we show that in stark contrast with utility-centric objectives, scoring is sub-optimal in achieving all utility-fairness trade-offs. We establish this with a series of counter-examples with a generic fairness formulation. We show that the issue persists whether we have a deterministic scoring function or a randomized one, or whether we measure fairness at the scope of a single query or across multiple queries. On the positive side, we empirically demonstrate that semi-greedy post-processing has the potential to achieve much better trade-offs, often approaching the ideal of exhaustive post-processing in a tractable way.

</details>


### [233] [TileMaxSim: IO-Aware GPU MaxSim Scoring with Dimension Tiling and Fused Product Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26439)
*Ashutosh Sharma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 为了解决 ColBERT 类检索模型中 GPU 计算因内存带宽受限（Memory-bound）而效率低下的问题，本文提出了 TileMaxSim。通过使用 Triton 内核优化内存读写、引入维度分块和融合积量化技术，该方案大幅提升了 GPU 带宽利用率，在保持检索精度的同时将评分吞吐量提升数百倍，显著降低了端到端系统延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多向量检索模型（如 ColBERT）虽然精度高，但其 GPU 实现效率低下，内存带宽利用率仅为 5-18%。这是因为直接计算并显式存储 Nq x Nd 相似度矩阵造成了巨大的内存访问浪费。

Method: 提出了 TileMaxSim 架构，包含三个核心优化点：1. 多查询 SRAM 分块（Tiling）：通过在共享内存中流式处理文档嵌入，减少 HBM 数据读取；2. 维度分块：支持处理维度大于 128 的嵌入向量；3. 融合积量化（PQ）评分：利用共享内存查询表减少 HBM I/O，最高可减少 31 倍。该方案基于 Triton 内核实现。

Result: 在 NVIDIA H100 GPU 上，TileMaxSim 的 HBM 带宽利用率达到 80.2%，文档评分吞吐量达 82M/s；相较于 loop-based 实现有 220 倍加速，相较于优化的 torch.compile 有 6.6-8.5 倍加速。在端到端检索任务中，将 100K 候选集的检索延迟从 268ms 降低至 1.2ms。

Conclusion: TileMaxSim 成功通过 I/O 感知优化解决了 ColBERT 等模型中 MaxSim 算子的内存带宽瓶颈，在保持原始检索精度的前提下，实现了相较于现有 CUDA/PyTorch 实现的显著性能提升（最高 220 倍），显著降低了检索系统的端到端延迟。

Abstract: Multi-vector retrieval models such as ColBERT achieve state-of-the-art accuracy through fine-grained token-level MaxSim scoring, yet existing GPU implementations leave most hardware performance unused. We give a roofline analysis of MaxSim on modern GPUs and identify a severe bandwidth gap: naive implementations reach only 5-18% of peak HBM bandwidth because they materialize the Nq x Nd similarity matrix, wasting memory traffic on data that is consumed once and discarded. We present TileMaxSim, a family of IO-aware Triton kernels that close this gap via (1) multi-query SRAM tiling that streams document embeddings through shared memory while accumulating per-query-token maxima in registers, reading each embedding from HBM exactly once; (2) dimension tiling that partitions the embedding dimension into 128-wide chunks, enabling scoring for d > 128 embeddings that overflow shared memory; and (3) fused product-quantization scoring via shared-memory lookup tables, cutting HBM I/O by up to ~31x. On NVIDIA H100 GPUs, TileMaxSim reaches 80.2% of peak HBM bandwidth and scores 82M documents/second (71.6M/s on real MS MARCO passages), a 220x speedup over loop-based scoring, 6.5x over fused PyTorch, 6.6-8.5x over torch.compile, and 469x the scoring throughput of WARP's CPU engine on the same node. TileMaxSim preserves exact retrieval quality: on MS MARCO and three BEIR benchmarks, rankings match reference MaxSim. As a drop-in replacement in ColBERTv2/PLAID, it cuts scoring latency at 100K candidates from 268 ms to 1.2 ms (98% lower end-to-end latency). We further show constant throughput from 100K to 500K documents, data-parallel multi-GPU sharding, robustness across dimensions 64-768, and FP16/BF16/FP32 support. Concurrent work independently develops an IO-aware fused MaxSim kernel; we differ in dimension tiling for d > 128 and fused product-quantization scoring.

</details>


### [234] [GPUSparse: GPU-Accelerated Learned Sparse Retrieval with Parallel Inverted Indices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26441)
*Ashutosh Sharma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: GPUSparse 是一种 GPU 加速的精确稀疏检索系统，通过创新的索引结构、批处理 Scatter-Add 算法和融合 Triton 内核，在保持 SPLADE 检索精度前提下，实现了相较于传统 CPU 检索方案数百倍的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 SPLADE 的学习型稀疏检索在检索质量上具有优势，但其实际部署严重依赖 CPU 端的倒排索引遍历（如 WAND 算法），导致在大规模实时场景下存在显著的计算瓶颈。

Method: 1. 设计了 GPU 并行的倒排索引结构（块对齐、Warp 合并访问）；2. 实现了批量化的 Scatter-Add 评分算法以支持大规模并发查询；3. 利用 Triton 构建融合算子，优化硬件资源利用率。

Result: 在 MS MARCO (8.8M 文档) 上，GPUSparse 实现了完全一致的精准评分（与 Pyserini 相比），速度提升 235 倍（1.27ms vs 298ms）；在 787 QPS 的吞吐量下，相较于牺牲精度换取速度的 Seismic 系统，获得了更高的精度；其核心算子达到 H100 峰值 HBM 带宽的 62.6%。

Conclusion: GPUSparse 通过 GPU 优化实现了高性能的精准稀疏检索，打破了 CPU 在实时大规模检索中的性能瓶颈，同时维持了与 CPU 方案完全一致的检索质量（MRR 和 Recall）。研究揭示了 GPU 稀疏检索中工作效率与硬件带宽利用率之间的权衡。

Abstract: Learned sparse retrieval models such as SPLADE achieve retrieval quality competitive with dense models while preserving the interpretability and exact-match advantages of sparse representations. However, inference-time scoring still relies on CPU-bound inverted index traversal algorithms (WAND, Block-Max WAND), creating a fundamental bottleneck for real-time serving at scale. We present GPUSparse, a system for GPU-accelerated exact learned sparse retrieval that introduces: (1) a GPU-parallel inverted index with block-aligned, warp-coalesced posting lists; (2) a batched scatter-add scoring algorithm that processes hundreds of queries simultaneously; and (3) fused Triton kernels with an analysis of the tradeoff between work-efficiency and hardware utilization. On MS MARCO passage ranking (8.8M passages) with real SPLADE embeddings, GPUSparse matches CPU exact scoring to three decimals (MRR@10=0.383, equal to Pyserini SPLADE at this precision; Recall@1000>=0.999 vs. dense matmul, the residual from floating-point tie-breaking) while providing a 235x speedup over Pyserini CPU at 8.8M documents (1.27ms vs. 298ms per query). Compared to Seismic (the fastest CPU sparse retrieval system), which trades 25% recall for speed (R@1000=0.738 vs. 0.983 exact), GPUSparse achieves exact scoring at 787 QPS throughput (batch 500) on the full 8.8M collection, with 1.3ms per query. Our document-parallel kernel reaches 62.6% of H100 peak HBM bandwidth, revealing a fundamental work-efficiency vs. bandwidth-efficiency tradeoff in GPU sparse retrieval. The reformulation of sparse scoring as scatter-add over an inverted index is shared with SPARe's iterative mode; our contribution is its fused-kernel realization, which we measure to be 23-270x faster than a faithful SPARe iterative reimplementation.

</details>


### [235] [Attributed, But Not Incremental: Cannibalization-Corrected Attribution for Large-Scale Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26690)
*Donghui Li,Bowen Yuan,Zili Yang,Qinxin Chen,Lijing Song*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对付费广告归因导致的“蚕食”夸大增长问题，本文提出了一种基于增量实验的自动化修正与分配框架，成功将因果实验的稀疏洞察转化为生产级别的精细化预算与流量优化决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在付费获取和增长体系中，传统的归因（如DNU）会因与自然流量、品牌流量等重叠而系统性地夸大增长，导致“归因-蚕食”错配，进而扭曲增量ROI的评估与预算分配决策。

Method: 提出了一个以增量实验为因果锚点的归因修正框架。首先利用因果增量实验获取跨时间的校准系数；其次，在满足结构一致性约束的条件下，将修正后的“蚕食量”从宏观层面分配到业务层级的不同细分维度，实现生产环境下的精细化应用。

Result: 离线验证显示，该框架在校准误差方面显著优于原始归因算法及细粒度的机器学习基线；在真实的生产环境部署中，帮助实现了约15个百分点的蚕食率降低。

Conclusion: 该框架通过跨层级分配修正量，将稀疏的因果实验数据转化为生产环境可用的精细化归因修正方案，显著提升了ROI测量的准确性，并成功在TikTok全球市场落地，有效降低了系统的蚕食率。

Abstract: In large-scale paid acquisition and growth advertising systems, production attribution outputs are widely used for daily budget allocation and channel diagnosis. However, paid-attributed conversions such as daily new users (DNU) may systematically overstate true incremental growth when paid channels overlap with organic demand, brand-driven traffic, or other acquisition channels. This attribution-cannibalization mismatch can distort incremental ROI measurement and budget decisions at scale.
  We propose an experiment-calibrated attribution correction framework that uses incrementality experiments as causal anchors to convert sparse lift measurements into daily correction estimates. To make the corrected signal actionable at production granularity, we further allocate calibrated cannibalization volume across business hierarchies under structural consistency constraints. Offline forward-in-time validation against channel-level incrementality experiment readouts shows that the proposed framework substantially reduces calibration error relative to raw attribution and fine-grained ML baselines. Deployed across multiple global TikTok markets, the system supported budget and traffic strategy adjustments that were followed by an approximately 15-percentage-point reduction in the measured cannibalization rate.

</details>


### [236] [A Shared IPTC Topic Space for Cross-Source Topic Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26845)
*Din Iskakov,Sebastian Gonçalves,Marco Idiat,Mendeli Vainstein,Aline Villavicencio,Ronaldo Menezes,Rodrigo Wilkens*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于IPTC taxonomy的共享主题空间生成框架，利用受引导的BERTopic方法将不同来源的文本映射至统一的主题分类，从而实现了跨媒体来源的可复现主题比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主题模型在不同语料库上独立训练时，会产生互不兼容的特定主题空间，导致难以跨媒体进行主题关注度的对比。因此需要一种能够将不同语料库统一在共享主题空间下的方法。

Method: 采用了受引导的 BERTopic（Guided BERTopic）模型。具体步骤包括：1. 利用94个IPTC一级分类作为外部标注点；2. 通过加权关键词和目标中心点对主题进行评分；3. 基于最大相似度原则将主题归并为17个IPTC父类；4. 在受控的《纽约时报》2011年语料库中进行了多阶段的开发与校准（包括模型筛选、映射细化及阈值校准）。

Result: 在测试语料库中，受引导模型在严格的分配阈值下，其覆盖率显著优于零样本基准（zero-shot benchmark）；父类增强的目标构建方式提升了主题覆盖率和分类的一致性；且随着赋值阈值的收紧，覆盖率表现出平缓下降而非断崖式下跌，验证了该方法的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该论文提供了一种基于外部本体（IPTC媒体主题分类法）的共享主题空间构建框架，能够有效解决不同语料库主题模型无法直接对比的难题，为跨媒体来源的主题比较提供了可复现的方法论。

Abstract: Comparing topic attention across different media is hindered by a fundamental modelling problem: topic models fitted separately to each corpus produce corpus-specific topic spaces that cannot be aligned directly. This paper presents a reproducible framework that places corpora in a single shared topic space defined by a taxonomy. Discovered topics are obtained with guided BERTopic, scored against the ninety-four IPTC Media Topics' taxonomy topics (level-1) through weighted keyword and target centroids, and then collapsed upward to seventeen IPTC parent topics by a maximum-similarity rule. The framework was developed and selected on a controlled New York Times 2011 corpus through a narrowing sequence: a broad model screen, a focused mapping refinement, a strict finalist comparison, a target-construction ablation, and a threshold calibration. In this corpus, the guided family retained substantially stronger mapped coverage than a zero-shot benchmark under stricter assignment thresholds, a parent-enriched target construction improved both coverage and parent consistency, and coverage declined gradually rather than collapsing as the assignment threshold was tightened. The contribution is an externally anchored method for constructing a shared topic space that enables reproducible cross-source topic comparison.

</details>


### [237] [TriPAH: Imbalance-Aware Tri-Prompt Affinity Hashing for Cross-Modal Medical Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27010)
*Jiaming Bian,Songming Li,Yurui Song,Yunfei Chen,Yichao Cao,Jun Long*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对医疗跨模态哈希中的语义碎片化难题，本文提出了 TriPAH 框架，通过引入本体知识增强的提示学习和多任务量化策略，实现了更精准的图像-文本特征对齐与高效的哈希编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗跨模态哈希方法面临临床语言噪声大、标签长尾分布以及量化过程脆弱（导致语义对齐能力减弱）等挑战，导致语义碎片化严重。

Method: 提出 TriPAH（Tri-Prompt Affinity Hashing）框架：1. 利用本体论引导的患者级提示（Ontology-grounded patient-level prompts）减少临床语言噪声；2. 使用轻量级提示令牌混合器进行多粒度对齐；3. 采用非对称多任务目标（Asymmetric multi-task objective），整合对比学习、不平衡分类及渐进式量化正则化；4. 引入患者级一致性模块 (Patient-level consistency module) 增强编码稳定性。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的广泛实验表明，TriPAH 在性能上显著优于当前最先进的方法（SOTA）。

Conclusion: TriPAH 框架通过融合本体知识和患者级提示，有效解决了医疗跨模态哈希检索中的语义碎片化和量化压力问题，在多个公开数据集上达到了业界领先水平。

Abstract: In the era of big medical data, efficient cross-modal retrieval is pivotal for evidence-based diagnosis and large-scale case management. Cross-modal medical hashing retrieval aims to enable efficient image-text search and support downstream tasks such as case-based reasoning and decision support by learning compact, semantically aligned binary codes. However, current methods suffer from semantic fragmentation due to noisy clinical language, long-tailed labels, and brittle quantization that weakens alignment. We propose TriPAH, a Tri-Prompt Affinity Hashing framework. TriPAH synthesizes ontology-grounded, patient-level prompts conditioned on normalized clinical cues to yield low-noise textual representations for initial alignment. A lightweight prompt-token mixer performs hierarchical, multi-granularity alignment and produces quantization-ready features under an asymmetric multi-task objective coupling multi-positive contrastive alignment, imbalance-aware classification, and progressive quantization regularization. A patient-level consistency module further stabilizes codes across complementary views. Extensive experiments on three public datasets demonstrate that TriPAH significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [238] [UniFormer: Efficient and Unified Model-Centric Scaling for Industrial Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27058)
*Bo Chen,Jinlong Jiao,Tijian Hu,Ruihao Zhang,Yanzhi Liu,Chenghou Jin,Qinglin Jia,Baixuan He,Hechang Pan,Yiwu Liu,Jian Liang,Chaoyi Ma,Ruiming Tang,Han Li,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: UniFormer 是一种针对工业推荐系统的统一模型架构，通过空间解耦、语义 Tokenization 和灵活的 FFN 参数缩放，克服了现有模型缩放效率低及行为模式建模难的问题，实现了性能与规模的双重突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统模型缩放（如 HyFormer, OneTrans）多局限于特征空间，缺乏跨组件的统一大规模缩放框架，且在追求高容量的同时容易导致推理效率下降及偏好塌陷。

Method: 1. 建模空间解耦：将整体空间划分为特征空间与任务空间，分别使用堆叠交互模块建模；2. 推理优化：引入基于语义的 Tokenization 实现用户-物品解耦，支持请求级推理加速；3. 模式建模：利用多序列交叉注意力捕捉异构行为模式，结合自注意力优化交互建模；4. 参数扩展：通过多视图 FFN 实现各建模组件的灵活、可扩展参数缩放。

Result: 在快手（Kuaishou）与快手极速版（Kuaishou Lite）的线上 A/B 测试中，UniFormer 取得显著效果：App 使用时长分别增长 0.101%/0.260%，观看时长分别增长 0.729%/1.113%。

Conclusion: UniFormer 通过创新的统一建模空间缩放框架，有效解决了工业级推荐系统中模型扩展与推理效率的矛盾，并在大规模工业实践中得到了显著的业务提升。

Abstract: Recently, substantial progress has been made in industrial recommendation through component-centric model scaling, where individual components such as behavior modeling, feature interaction, or task modeling are independently scaled to improve model capacity. Although recent methods such as HyFormer and OneTrans further explore cross-module co-scaling by jointly modeling behavior and interaction, their designs are still confined to the feature space and lack a unified model-centric scaling framework over the overall modeling space. In this paper, we propose UniFormer, an efficient and unified model-centric scaling framework for industrial recommender systems. To improve efficiency, UniFormer decomposes the overall modeling space into feature and task spaces, which are modeled by stacked Feature-space Interaction Modules and Task-space Interaction Modules, respectively. Moreover, UniFormer introduces semantic-based tokenization scheme to enable user-item decoupling, thereby achieving request-level inference acceleration. To prevent preference collapse, UniFormer employs multi-sequence cross-attention to separately capture heterogeneous behavior patterns, followed by the self-attention to enhance interaction modeling. Besides, dedicated multi-view FFNs are introduced to support flexible and scalable parameter scaling across different modeling components. Extensive online A/B testing in two production scenarios, Kuaishou and Kuaishou Lite, shows that UniFormer consistently improves user engagement and interaction metrics, achieving gains of +0.101%/+0.260% in App Stay Time and +0.729%/+1.113% in Watch Time, respectively.

</details>


### [239] [TRUST: Item-Calibrated Interval Evidence for Temporal Session-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27214)
*Linjiang Guo,Nitin Bisht,Shiqing Wu,Yifan Yin,Guandong Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对现有会话推荐系统忽视物品间时间间隔分布差异的问题，作者提出了TRUST框架，通过引入基于物品分布的时间间隔相对校准机制，显著提升了包括邻居采样和图编码在内的多个推荐环节的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于会话的推荐系统通常将时间间隔视为绝对值，假设不同物品具有等同的时间语义。作者通过实证研究发现该假设不成立，即每个物品具有独立的时间间隔分布，因此需要将时间间隔相对于其所属的物品进行相对评估。

Method: 提出了一个评估框架TRUST，核心在于将观测到的时间间隔与每个物品特定的经验时间间隔分布进行对比。该方法设计了一个评分函数，用于指导三个关键环节：全局邻居采样、会话图编码以及最终的兴趣聚合。此外，该方法具有模型无关性，可作为插件嵌入现有模型。

Result: 在多个公共数据集上的实验表明，TRUST在时间序列和非时间序列基线模型上均表现出色；作为插件使用时，能有效提升现有时间序列推荐模型的性能。消融实验证实，模块化的时间信号校准能显著优化邻居采样、图编码及兴趣聚合效果。

Conclusion: 提出了一种名为TRUST的框架，通过对时间间隔进行个性化的相对校准，证明了该方法在处理物品特定时间间隔分布时的有效性，并验证了其作为通用模块能提升现有时间序列会话推荐算法的潜力。

Abstract: Temporal signals have been widely used in session-based recommendation to infer user interest. Existing temporal session-based recommenders primarily rely on absolute interval values, implicitly assuming that the same interval carries similar interest signals across items. However, we empirically find that this assumption does not hold: each item has its own interval distribution, so an interval should be interpreted relative to the item it belongs to. Based on this observation, we propose TRUST, a framework that evaluates each observed interval relative to the empirical interval distribution of the corresponding item. Specifically, we propose a score function to guide global neighbor sampling, session graph encoding, and final interest aggregation. Experiments on public datasets show that TRUST consistently improves over representative temporal and non-temporal baselines, and plug-in experiments further show that the proposed scoring function can improve existing temporal session recommenders as a model-agnostic method. Component-wise ablations further show that calibrating the temporal signals within each module, rather than removing the module itself, consistently improves neighbor sampling, session graph encoding, and interest aggregation.

</details>


### [240] [NOVA: A Verification-Aware Agent Harness for Architecture Evolution in Industrial Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27243)
*Shaohua Liu,Liang Fang,Yilong Sun,Shudong Huang,Qingsong Luo,Xiaoyang Chen,Dongqiang Liu,Chuangang Ma,Zhenzhen Chai,Henghuan Wang,Shijie Quan,Changyuan Cui,Zhangbin Zhu,Peng Chen,Wei Xu,Lei Xiao,Haijie Gu,Jie Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了 NOVA，这是一个包含智能验证机制和分级任务控制的自动架构进化智能体，解决了传统推荐模型开发中专家依赖重、迭代效率低及静默故障多等核心挑战，并在生产环境中实现了显著的业务指标提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业广告推荐模型的架构演进对业务增长至关重要，但手动开发门槛高、难以扩展；现有的 AutoML 仅限于超参数微调，而通用的 LLM 编码智能体缺乏对推荐系统架构有效性的评估，容易产生通过编译但性能不佳的“静默故障”。

Method: 提出了一种名为 NOVA 的多层级智能体框架：1. 使用“架构梯度”（Architecture Gradient）作为非微分更新信号，结合历史修改、验证诊断和指标反馈指导模型优化；2. 建立“验证级联”（Verification Cascade）机制，通过多维度约束（语义、可执行性、离线/在线效果）过滤无效候选结构；3. 实施 L1-L4 任务分级控制，针对不同复杂度任务匹配自动化程度与人工监管需求。

Result: 在工业广告系统中，NOVA 在 L2 和 L3 任务的有效通过率分别达到 54.5% 和 60.0%；相比传统编码智能体大幅降低了静默故障率；将从文献到落地的生产周期缩短了 13 倍以上；在线 A/B 测试中，显著提升了 GMV（最高 +2.02%）并有效降低了 pCVR 偏差（最高 66.7%）。

Conclusion: NOVA 证明了一种包含架构梯度反馈、多层级验证级联和风险分级控制的智能体框架，能有效解决工业推荐系统架构演进中的开发门槛高、错误率高和迭代周期长的问题。

Abstract: Industrial advertising recommender models are continuously improved through architecture evolution. Upgrades such as RankMixer, TokenMixer-Large, and MixFormer show that better structures remain a key source of quality and business gains. Yet developing such upgrades in production is expert-intensive and difficult to scale. Existing automation is insufficient: AutoML mainly tunes hyper-parameters, while effective gains often require cross-module changes under strict constraints; generic LLM coding agents optimize for runnable code, but runnable code does not imply a valid recommender architecture. Candidates may pass local tests while causing silent failures that degrade performance.
  We present NOVA, a level-aware agent harness for verification-aware architecture evolution. NOVA uses an architecture gradient, an SGD-inspired, non-differentiable update signal that aggregates prior modifications, verification diagnostics, metric feedback, and trajectory memory to guide the next modification. A verification cascade checks structure semantics, local executability, offline effectiveness, and online impact; invalid candidates are blocked early, with failure patterns recorded as forbidden directions. L1--L4 task-level control matches automation to task complexity and risk, routing high-risk tasks to Copilot for human oversight. Deployed in an industrial advertising system, NOVA achieves the highest effective pass rate on L2 ScaleUp and L3 Literature-to-Production tasks (54.5% and 60.0%), reduces silent failures compared with coding-agent baselines, and shortens one literature-to-production cycle by over 13x in human-attended time. In online A/B testing, the selected L3 candidate improves GMV on three pCVR objectives by +1.25%, +1.70%, and +2.02%, while reducing pCVR bias by 58.8%, 66.7%, and 37.3%.

</details>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [241] [On-Demand Service Zone Design for Energy-Constrained Spatial Queueing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26765)
*Peng Lin,Cheng Hua,Wei Qi,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 针对受电池约束的电动服务车辆（ESVs）运营问题，通过构建新的排队模型和求解框架，证明了精细的区域服务设计比盲目提升电池容量对提升利润与响应可靠性更具战略价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动服务车辆（ESVs）因受电池限制和充电需求影响，其调度可行性与空间部署面临挑战，现有的传统空间服务器模型无法有效刻画这些特征。

Method: 1. 开发了嵌入电池状态动态的能量约束超立方空间排队模型（基于半马尔可夫表示）；2. 提出了站址布局与服务区域设计的联合优化问题；3. 开发了针对外部可计算列系数的“分支-定价-评估”（Branch-Price-and-Evaluation）框架。

Result: 显式能量建模能显著减少虚假服务承诺，提供更可靠的规划决策；揭示了负载依赖的区域划分逆转现象（轻需求下适合池化，高需求下适合紧凑分区）；利润提升主要受区域规划驱动而非电池容量改善。

Conclusion: 能源约束不仅是电池容量问题，更是决定服务区域配置的关键维度。研究强调在投资电池升级前，应优先优化服务区域设计；大容量电池在稀疏需求下可能适得其反，减缓补能速度并降低车队就绪度。

Abstract: Electric service vehicles (ESVs), such as mobile chargers and drone-based service units, are becoming an important operational resource for on-demand service systems. Unlike conventional spatial servers, ESV operations are shaped by battery limits and recharging needs, which affect dispatch feasibility and spatial deployment decisions. We develop an energy-constrained hypercube spatial queueing model that embeds battery-state dynamics into the classical hypercube framework and uses a semi-Markov representation to estimate steady-state performance. We then formulate a joint location--zoning problem for station placement and service zone design. The resulting large-scale mixed-integer nonlinear program admits a set partitioning reformulation whose column coefficients are not available in closed form. We therefore develop a Branch-Price-and-Evaluation framework for set partitioning problems with externally computable column coefficients: upper-bounding surrogates guide pricing, and iterative exact evaluation updates the coefficients of active columns. Computational results show that explicit energy modeling significantly reduces false service promises and yields more credible planning decisions. They also reveal a load-dependent reversal in zoning: pooling is preferable under light demand, whereas tighter zoning becomes more profitable as demand increases. Over the tested range, profitability is driven more by zoning than by battery improvement, suggesting that managers should get service zone design right before investing in battery upgrades; this caution is reinforced by the counterintuitive finding that larger batteries may delay replenishment and reduce fleet readiness under sparse demand. These findings show that energy feasibility is not merely a matter of battery-capacity expansion, but a design dimension that shapes service-zone configuration.

</details>

### [242] [Structural parameterizations of Geodetic Set on directed (acyclic) graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26414)
*Beaudou Laurent,Foucaud Florent,Lorieau Lucas,Tale Prafullkumar*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了有向测地集问题（DIRECTED GEODETIC SET）的参数化复杂度。通过分析顶点覆盖数、最大度及可达直径等参数，提供了最优的核化算法和时间复杂度上界，并结合ETH证明了相关下界，同时确定了该问题在多种约束条件下的硬度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DIRECTED GEODETIC SET（有向测地集）问题在参数化复杂度分析中已知在参数 $k$ 下是 W[2]-hard 的。为了寻求更高效的求解途径，本文旨在探究针对其他结构化参数（如顶点覆盖数、最大度、可达直径）的参数化算法可行性及其复杂度下界。

Method: 1. 核化分析：推导了基于顶点覆盖数 ($vcn$) 和组合参数 ($(k, Δ, rdiam)$) 的核化算法。
2. 算法设计：利用核化结论设计了相应的参数化算法。
3. 下界证明：基于指数时间假设 (ETH) 证明了所提出的算法在$vcn$及组合参数下是最优的。
4. 硬度证明：通过规约证明了在特定约束参数（如最大度、可达直径）下的W[2]-hard和para-NP-hard性质。

Result: 1. 给出了 $vcn$ 参数下的 $2^{O(vcn)}$ 大小核和 $2^{O(vcn^2)} \cdot n^{O(1)}$ 时间算法，且证明该时间复杂度在ETH下最优。
2. 给出了基于 $(k, Δ, rdiam)$ 的 $(kΔ)^{O(rdiam)}$ 大小核及相应时间算法，同样证明该复杂度在ETH下最优。
3. 证明了该问题在参数 $k$ 下即便在最大度为3的图上仍为 W[2]-hard，且在参数 (最大度, 可达直径) 下为 para-NP-hard。

Conclusion: DIRECTED GEODETIC SET问题在参数化复杂性上具有内在的困难性。通过建立各种参数下的核化结果及其匹配的ETH下界，揭示了该问题在参数$vcn$以及$(k, Δ, rdiam)$组合下的精确复杂性，并证明了即便在DAG或受限度数/直径的图上，该问题依然难以解决。

Abstract: In DIRECTED GEODETIC SET, we are given a (directed) graph and seek a small solution set $S \subseteq V(G)$ such that every vertex lies on a shortest directed path between two vertices in $S$.
  It is known that the problem is W[2]-hard when parameterized by the solution size $k$, even on directed acyclic graphs (DAGs).
  Our first result is a kernel of size $2^{O(vcn)}$ for DIRECTED GEODETIC SET on general digraphs, where $vcn$ denotes the vertex cover number of the underlying (undirected) graph. This implies an algorithm running in time $2^{O(vcn^2)} \cdot n^{O(1)}$. Furthermore, we prove that, assuming the ETH, the problem does not admit an algorithm running in time $2^{o(vcn^2)} \cdot n^{O(1)}$. Next, we show that on general digraphs, DIRECTED GEODETIC SET admits a natural kernel of size $(kΔ)^{O(rdiam)}$, where $Δ$ is the maximum degree and $rdiam$ denotes the reachability diameter of the digraph (a natural analogue of diameter of undirected graphs). This yields an algorithm running in time $(kΔ)^{O(rdiam \cdot k)}\cdot n^{O(1)}$. We further prove that, assuming the ETH, the problem does not admit an algorithm running in time $(kΔ)^{o(rdiam \cdot k)} \cdot n^{O(1)}$. Finally, we justify the necessity of combining parameters by establishing the following hardness results for DIRECTED GEODETIC SET:
  - It is W[2]-hard parameterized by $k$, even on digraphs of maximum degree 3.
  - It is para-NP-hard parameterized by maximum degree and reachability diameter.
  One can infer that the problem remains W[2]-hard when parameterized by k, even on graphs of reachability diameter 3 from Araújo and Arraes [DAM 2022].
  All our conditional lower bounds and hardness results hold even when the input digraph is restricted to be a DAG.

</details>


### [243] [Almost Optimal Multiple Source Shortest Paths and Reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26554)
*Barna Saha,Yinzhan Xu,Christopher Ye*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对图论中的多源最短路径和可达性问题，利用新型图分解技术，通过将复杂度与矩形矩阵乘法（BMM）对齐，实现了近乎最优的运行时间，填补了该领域长期存在的性能空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多源最短路径（MSSP）和多源可达性算法在面对不同数量的源顶点集合（$n^\sigma$）时，运行时间在 $\hat{O}(n^\omega)$（全源）和 $\hat{O}(n^{2+\sigma})$（多次单源）之间存在显著的性能鸿沟，未能达到矩形矩阵乘法（BMM）的理论下界。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的图分解技术作为核心工具，将MSSP与BMM复杂度挂钩，实现了从单源到全源的最短路径算法的平滑插值，并将其泛化至DAG，最终应用于直径缩减问题中的快捷集合（shortcut set）计算。

Result: 在无向无权图中实现了 $\hat{O}(n^{\omega(\sigma, 1, 1)})$ 的MSSP算法；在有向图中实现了相同时间复杂度的多源可达性算法；并推导出了一种 $O(n^{2.084})$ 时间复杂度的快捷集合（shortcut set）构建算法。

Conclusion: 本文提供了多源最短路径（MSSP）和多源可达性问题的近乎最优算法，在无向无权图和有向无环图（DAG）上实现了与矩形矩阵乘法（BMM）相同的运行时间复杂度 $\hat{O}(n^{\omega(\sigma, 1, 1)})$，弥补了先前算法在不同规模源集下的性能差距。

Abstract: Given a graph, computing distances and reachabilities from a small set of vertices to the whole graph is an important primitive both in theory and in practice.
  In undirected unweighted graphs, while computing single-source shortest path (SSSP) requires $O(n^2)$ time in dense graphs, all-pairs shortest paths (APSP) can be computed in $\hat{O}(n^ω) = O(n^{2.372})$ time [Seidel '95] providing significant savings over running $n$ SSSP instances separately. However, if one needs to compute multiple-source shortest paths (MSSP) from a set of $n^σ$ vertices, the previously best known running time was $\hat{O}(\min\{n^ω, n^{2 + σ}\})$: either compute APSP or run SSSP from each source. On the other hand, MSSP is only as hard as computing Boolean matrix product (BMM) between an $n^σ\times n$ matrix and $n \times n$ matrix, leaving a significant gap. Our first main result is an almost optimal algorithm for MSSP on undirected unweighted graphs running in $\hat{O}(n^{ω(σ, 1, 1)})$ time, which gives a smooth interpolation between the SSSP and APSP algorithms. The main technical tool behind our result is a novel graph decomposition, which may be of independent interest.
  Next, we study the multiple-source reachability problem, where we need to determine whether a given set of $n^σ$ vertices can reach each of the vertices in a given directed graph. Multiple-source reachability can also be solved in $\hat{O}(\min\{n^ω, n^{2 + σ}\})$ time, with the same lower bound from rectangular BMM. We give an optimal algorithm that runs in $\hat{O}(n^{ω(σ, 1, 1)})$ time, again matching the running time for BMM. Our algorithm for multiple-source reachability can be generalized to MSSP on DAGs. As an application, we provide an $O(n^{2.084})$ time algorithm for computing an $\widetilde{O}(n)$-size shortcut set that reduces diameter to $O(n^{1/3})$.

</details>


### [244] [Fast Enumeration of Minimal Removable Sets in Monotone Systems with Application to Core Collapse Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26639)
*Kan Shota,Kazuya Haraguchi*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于单调系统的通用框架，用于高效枚举$k$-core网络中的最小可移除集（MinRS），并通过引入特定属性实现了$O((n+m)\log n)$的时间复杂度，显著提升了针对各种$k$-core变体进行鲁棒性分析的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $k$-core结构在网络脆弱性分析中容易发生“核心坍塌”（core collapse），即少量顶点的移除可能导致核心尺寸显著下降。寻找并枚举导致这种坍塌的“最小可移除集”（MinRS）对于理解网络鲁棒性至关重要。

Method: 基于单调系统（monotone systems）的通用数学框架；定义了“in-dominating seed”属性以优化递归搜索效率；利用该属性将复杂问题的枚举时间复杂度降低。

Result: 1. 通过通用框架实现了所有MinRS的枚举，基础复杂度为$O((n+m)n \tau_\omega)$；2. 在满足“in-dominating seed”属性时，复杂度优化至$O((n+m)\log n \cdot \tau_\omega)$；3. 证明了无向图中的标准$k$-core满足该属性，使得枚举复杂度达到$O((n+m)\log n)$；4. 提出了一种针对所有$k$-core子图枚举的延迟算法，其延迟优于Boley等人（2010）的方法。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种通用且高效的框架，用于枚举给定$k$-core中的所有最小可移除集（MinRS）及所有$k$-core子图。通过引入“in-dominating seed”属性，将$k$-core中MinRS的枚举复杂度优化至$O((n+m)\log n)$，并在多种$k$-core扩展模型中均保持高效性。

Abstract: In network vulnerability analysis, it is crucial to evaluate the robustness of $k$-cores against vertex removals. A $k$-core is often fragile since removing a few vertices can trigger a large reduction in the core size, a phenomenon known as core collapse. In this paper, we study the problem of enumerating all minimal removable sets (MinRSs) of a given $k$-core, where a MinRS is a minimal nonempty set of vertices whose removal results in a smaller $k$-core graph. We consider this problem within a general mathematical framework based on monotone systems. We show that, for a monotone system that is given with an underlying graph $G=(V,E)$, all MinRSs of a solution can be enumerated in $O((n+m)nτ_ω)$ time, where $n=|V|$, $m=|E|$ and $τ_ω$ denotes the computation time of evaluating the monotone function of the system. Furthermore, if the system satisfies the newly defined in-dominating seed property, the complexity drops to $O((n+m) \log n \cdot τ_ω)$ time. We prove that standard $k$-cores in undirected graphs satisfy this property, enabling MinRS enumeration in $O((n+m)\log n)$ time, a significant improvement over the baseline. We also extend our framework to enumerate all solutions in a given monotone system. This yields an $O((n+m)\log n)$-delay algorithm for all $k$-core subgraphs, outperforming an algorithm given by [Boley et al., Theoretical Computer Science, 2010]. Our framework is applicable to various $k$-core extensions, including weighted $k$-cores, multi-layer $\boldsymbol{k}$-cores, and $(k,\ell)$-cores.

</details>


### [245] [Fast algorithms for learning a Gaussian under halfspace truncation with optimal sample complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27298)
*Haitong Liu,Deepak Narayanan Sridharan,David Steurer,Manuel Wiedmer*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文针对高维半空间截断高斯分布的学习问题，提出了一种基于“相对截断参数”的新方法，实现了理论最优的样本和时间复杂度，无需使用传统的代价高昂的梯度下降方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对高维半空间截断高斯分布的学习问题，现有研究（如 Lee 等人 FOCS'24 的工作）虽然实现了多项式时间算法，但在样本和计算复杂度上尚未达到最优，作者旨在寻找一种更高效的最优解法。

Method: 通过提出一种基于“相对截断参数”（relative truncation parameter）的低阶矩重解释方法，绕过了传统的投影随机梯度下降（PSGD）过程，实现了对未截断高斯参数的直接恢复。

Result: 在目标精度 $\varepsilon$ 和维度 $d$ 下，实现了 $n = \tilde{O}(d^2/\varepsilon^2)$ 的样本复杂度，且运行时间主要由计算经验协方差矩阵的开销决定，在收敛性能上与无截断情况下的高斯学习相当。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种关于截断高斯分布的学习算法，该算法在样本复杂度和计算复杂度上均达到了最优（$O(d^2/\varepsilon^2)$ 样本，且计算复杂度与计算经验协方差矩阵相当），实现了在半空间截断下学习高斯分布的“代价为零”特性。

Abstract: We study the fundamental problem of learning a high-dimensional Gaussian truncated to an unknown halfspace. Lee, Mehrotra and Zampetakis (FOCS'24) recently obtained the first polynomial time algorithm for this problem, but their resulting sample and time complexity bounds are not optimal. Under non-trivial truncation, for any target accuracy $\varepsilon > 0$ and dimension $d$ we give an efficient algorithm that uses $n = \tilde{O}(d^2/\varepsilon^2)$ samples and learns the underlying Gaussian to error $\varepsilon$ in total variation distance. Our algorithm is also fast: its runtime is dominated by the cost of computing the empirical covariance matrix. Both our sample and time complexity are optimal in terms of $d$ and $\varepsilon$ even without truncation: in this regard, we can learn a Gaussian under halfspace truncation for free.
  The key ingredient behind our result is a novel reinterpretation of the low-degree moments of the truncated Gaussian in terms of a relative truncation parameter. This relative truncation parameter uniquely determines the parameters of the untruncated Gaussian and enables direct parameter recovery. This reinterpretation allows us to circumvent the time intensive projected stochastic gradient descent procedure that is widely used in learning under truncation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [246] [Mitigating High-Frequency Geometric Noise in Non-Parametric 1-Bit Sparse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26137)
*Lars Kopp*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需乘法的类脑计算框架，通过双流形投影与硬阈值化将数据转换为稀疏二值编码。针对重构过程中出现的几何噪声，研究提出了硬件级低通滤波方案，实现了高精度的信号恢复，为边缘AI提供了一种高效、稳定的计算架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决传统深度学习中高功耗浮点运算的问题，探索无需乘法的类脑计算数据编码范式，以满足边缘AI对能效的需求。

Method: 提出了一种确定性、非参数的双流形执行框架，将128维整数向量映射到1024维超完备空间，采用8位有界变换矩阵和硬激活阈值，生成稀疏的1位二值种群编码。并引入硬件级数字低通滤波器以抑制线性重构过程中产生的几何噪声。

Result: 指出并解决了非参数映射中的几何噪声问题；揭示了输入函数复杂度与重构误差之间的矛盾；实验证明低开销数字低通滤波器可有效消除噪声，在不同复杂度和阈值条件下均表现出近乎零重构误差的稳定性。

Conclusion: 该架构验证了一种极其稳定、无需乘法的类脑计算方案，对于边缘AI应用具有重要价值。通过简单的硬件数字低通滤波器即可消除几何噪声，实现近乎零误差的信号重构。

Abstract: Energy-efficient neuromorphic computing requires alternative data-encoding paradigms that bypass power-hungry floating-point operations. This paper evaluates a deterministic, non-parametric dual-manifold execution framework that maps dense 128-element integer vectors - representing digitized multi-frequency trigonometric waveforms - into a 1024-dimensional overcomplete space using 8-bit bounded transformation matrices. By enforcing a hard activation threshold, the system yields an ultra-sparse, 1-bit binary population code where y belongs to the set (0, 1)^1024. We identify and address a critical phenotypic artifact of this non-parametric mapping: the emergence of high-frequency geometric noise during linear reconstruction. Furthermore, we document an algorithmic complexity paradox where low-complexity input functions yield significantly higher reconstruction errors than highly complex, high-degree trigonometric combinations. Because the underlying basis functions operate as purely objective mathematical entities without statistical priors regarding signal smoothness, this geometric noise is proven to be strictly orthogonal to the core signal topology. Consequently, we demonstrate that a low-overhead, hardware-level digital low-pass filter completely eliminates this artifact, reducing reconstruction errors to near-zero bounds even under tight overcompleteness constraints. This architecture validates a highly stable, multiplier-free alternative to traditional deep learning hardware for edge-AI applications, verified through comprehensive empirical evaluations across varying complexity scales and classification thresholds (tau = 10 and tau = 100).

</details>


### [247] [The Kernel's Write: Application Read-Only Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26138)
*Hui Sub Shim,Katherine Mohr,Philip Levis*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 针对LtRAM硬件层管理带来的高延迟问题，本文提出了AROM（应用只读内存）架构，通过利用操作系统的写时复制（CoW）机制来管理LtRAM写入，从而简化硬件设计并显著提升执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LtRAM（如Intel Optane）相比DRAM拥有高密度和低成本优势，但存在写入延迟高、寿命限制及写粒度粗的问题；现有的硬件翻译层实现方式会显著增加延迟，限制了性能。

Method: 引入AROM机制：将LtRAM页面设为应用只读；利用写时复制（CoW）处理应用写操作：当应用尝试写入LtRAM时触发缺页异常，将页面迁回DRAM后再执行写操作；将复杂的管理逻辑（地址重映射、缓存等）从硬件控制器迁移至操作系统。

Result: 通过简化硬件设计并由OS接管内存管理，理论上能够在读密集型工作负载中实现与纯DRAM相当的性能，同时克服了传统LtRAM控制器带来的额外延迟。

Conclusion: 通过利用AROM（应用只读内存）和写时复制（CoW）机制，将LtRAM的管理职责从硬件控制器卸载到操作系统，从而在保持LtRAM高密度和低成本优势的同时，有效避免了硬件复杂性带来的延迟开销，实现了接近纯DRAM的读性能。

Abstract: Alongside power, DRAM has become a major limiting factor in datacenter growth. As DRAM's cost-per-bit has plateaued over the past decade, a class of emerging memory technologies, called Long-term RAM (LtRAM), offers a path to denser and cheaper main memory.
  However, LtRAM has three main drawbacks: asymmetric read/write latencies, limited endurance, and coarse write granularity. In an attempt to isolate software from these drawbacks, LtRAM technologies such as Intel Optane copy an approach from flash devices and introduce a translation layer that manages wear-leveling, address remapping, and read/write caching. Prior experimental studies have found these operations add significantly to LtRAM latency.
  Rather than making LtRAM look like DRAM, we propose redesigning the hardware/software interface to offload more responsibility to the operating system. This design hinges on one central property, Application Read-Only Memory (AROM): LtRAM pages are read-only to applications and written only by the OS during page migrations. AROM is enforced by leveraging copy-on-write (CoW): application writes to LtRAM trigger a fault that migrates the page back to DRAM before the store is applied. This invariant allows us to shift LtRAM management from the on-DIMM controller to the operating system, drastically simplifying the DIMM's hardware. With this approach, we aim to match the performance of pure DRAM on read-mostly workloads while delivering LtRAM's density and cost advantages.

</details>


### [248] [Toward Mitigating Process-Induced Performance Degradation in 3.5D Heterogeneous Packages via Pre-Silicon Firmware Co-Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26176)
*Chi Fei Chung,Nikolai Nedovodin*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对英特尔3.5D异构封装的物理感知固件调度层（XRM-SSD），通过预测性的热管理策略，显著提升了算力效率并降低了设计裕量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决英特尔3.5D异构集成封装（Foveros Direct 3D, PowerVia, EMIB-T, UCIe, HBM5等）在复杂工作负载下的热管理与物理性能限制问题。

Method: 提出XRM-SSD V24/V7.0固件调度层，采用基于物理感知的预测控制；利用热-电协同仿真进行90,000步LLM推理数据集验证；通过主动式负载密度导向的热提示（20-50ms预判）实现PowerVia电压轨预置；采用N x N热耦合矩阵和双极核处理多Tile架构，并通过蒙特卡洛分析验证鲁棒性。

Result: 热负载相关性达到R^2=0.9911；CPO光谱漂移补偿至0.36nm（仅占TSMC公差预算的21%）；HBM漏电流被稳定控制；实现20-30%的算力释放及65-68%的EDA设计裕量（Guard-band）缩减。

Conclusion: 固件与硬件的协同优化（Firmware-Hardware Co-optimization）是突破先进3.5D封装物理限制的有效途径，能够显著提升算力利用率并减少EDA设计裕量。

Abstract: This paper presents a pre-silicon analysis of XRM-SSD V24/V7.0, a physics-aware predictive firmware scheduling layer for Intel's 3.5D heterogeneous integrated packages (Foveros Direct 3D + PowerVia + EMIB-T + UCIe + HBM5). Using detailed thermal-electrical co-simulation over a 90,000-step LLM inference dataset, we show that proactive workload-density-driven thermal hinting (20-50 ms look-ahead) enables pre-positioning of PowerVia voltage rails. Key results include a thermal-load correlation of R^2 = 0.9911, compensated CPO spectral drift below 0.36 nm (21% of TSMC tolerance budget), and HBM leakage current clamped below 1 MB/hr across all load states. Monte Carlo analysis (N=2,000 trials) confirms robustness under process variation. V7.0 extends the framework to multi-tile architectures with an N x N thermal coupling matrix and two-pole kernel. The approach demonstrates potential for 20-30% released compute and 65-68% EDA guard-band reduction. All metrics are engineering projections from pre-silicon characterization. Silicon validation on Intel 18A platforms is pending. This work highlights firmware-hardware co-optimization as an effective approach to mitigating physical limits in advanced 3.5D packaging.

</details>


### [249] [CVA6-RT: an Open-Source Time-Predictable RV64 Processor for Mixed-Criticality Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26177)
*Enrico Zelioli,Christopher Reinwardt,Nils Wistoff,Robert Balas,Alessandro Ottaviano,Luca Benini,Angelo Garofalo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出 CVA6-RT，通过 TLB 管理、可重配置缓存和增强中断机制，显著降低了 RISC-V CVA6 核心的执行抖动及中断延迟，使其在保持高性能的同时具备硬实时确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 CVA6 等高性能 RISC-V 核心在处理实时任务时，受限于不可控的缓存抖动和延迟抖动，难以满足硬实时系统的确定性要求。

Method: 提出了 CVA6-RT 架构，引入了以下关键机制：1. TLB 分区与锁定（保障地址转换确定性）；2. 可动态重配置的 L1 Cache 暂存存储器 (Scratchpad) 模式（提升访存确定性）；3. 增强型中断控制器与硬件辅助上下文堆栈（优化中断响应性能）。

Result: 在开启实时特性后，CVA6-RT 的中断延迟降至 12 个周期，与低端 Arm Cortex-M 微控制器相当，相比原始 CVA6 核心提升了 10 倍。

Conclusion: CVA6-RT 通过微架构优化，成功在维持 RV64GCH 指令集支持的同时，实现了可预测的实时性能，从而弥补了高性能 RISC-V 核心在实时性方面的不足。

Abstract: This work presents CVA6-RT, a real-time micro-architectural extension of the CVA6 core to bound worst-case latency and reduce task's timing execution variability.
