CVA6-RT implements the rv64gch ISA and features advanced support for real-time execution, including TLB partitioning and locking for predictable address translation, a dynamically reconfigurable scratchpad mode in the L1 caches for deterministic memory access, and low-latency interrupt handling via an enhanced interrupt controller combined with hardware-assisted context stacking. With real-time features enabled, CVA6-RT achieves an interrupt latency of 12 cycles, comparable to that of simpler Arm Cortex-M microcontrollers, and 10x lower than the baseline CVA6 core.

</details>


### [250] [Nanoelectromechanical Systems (NEMS) for Hardware Security in Advanced Packaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26426)
*Himanandhan Reddy Kottur,Pavanbabu Arjunamahanthi,M. Shafkat M. Khan,Liton Kumar Biswas,Nitin Varshney,Navid Asadizanjani*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出利用纳米机电系统（NEMS）通过物理不可克隆特性和独特的机械机理，为半导体硬件提供防篡改、防伪及高安全性的验证机制，以应对全产业链的安全挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对半导体制造和先进封装过程中日益严重的篡改、伪造及供应链渗透等硬件安全威胁，需要新型物理机制来提供硬件级防护。

Method: 利用NEMS的非确定性和制造工艺产生的纳米级随机差异，构建PUF（物理不可克隆函数）、形状记忆材料、谐振指纹以及物理锁定架构。

Result: 证明了基于NEMS的硬件安全机制在抗环境退化、防逆向工程及提升安全性方面优于传统数字方案。

Conclusion: NEMS技术是一种高安全、低功耗的硬件安全原语，能有效防御逆向工程和侧信道攻击，为半导体供应链安全提供了可扩展且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: As hardware security threats escalate across semiconductor manufacturing and advanced packaging, there is a growing need for novel physical mechanisms to counter sophisticated attacks such as tampering, counterfeiting, and supply chain infiltration. This paper presents Nanoelectromechanical Systems (NEMS) as an emerging class of hardware security primitives that enable physical assurance, tamper detection, and authentication at the device level. Leveraging mechanisms such as NEMS-based Physically Unclonable Functions (PUFs), shape memory materials, resonance-based fingerprints, and physical unlocking architectures, these systems offer enhanced resilience to reverse engineering, side-channel attacks, and environmental degradation. By harnessing mechanical unpredictability and fabrication-induced nanoscale variability, NEMS technologies introduce a physically robust and low-power alternative to conventional digital security methods. Their seamless integration into standard semiconductor workflows paves the way for scalable, verifiable, and secure solutions across defense, aerospace, critical infrastructure, and consumer electronics.

</details>


### [251] [GRAINS: Storage-Aware Algorithm-Architecture Co-Design Enabling High-Performance and Low-Cost Graph-Based Genome Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26468)
*Nika Mansouri Ghiasi,Harun Mustafa,Talu Güloglu,Rakesh Nadig,Konstantina Koliogeorgi,Susana Rebolledo Ruiz,Marc Rautmann,Furkan Eris,Mohammad Sadrosadati,Jisung Park,Onur Mutlu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 为了解决大规模基因组图分析中严重的数据移动开销问题，论文提出了 GRAINS——首个存储内基因组图分析系统，通过算法与架构的协同设计，实现了在存储设备内部高效处理基因组图数据，显著提升了计算性能与能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模基因组图数据处理存在严重的数据移动瓶颈，而现有分析工具受限于SSD内部有限的硬件资源，且过往的存储中心化系统无法适配基因组图独特的结构与访问模式，因此迫切需要一种能够适配存储环境的基因组图分析框架。

Method: 提出了一种针对基因组图分析的存储感知算法与架构协同设计方案，具体包括：1. 基于基因组图特性的新型批处理与执行流；2. 利用闪存内与存储内处理技术减少低重用数据的传输；3. 一种轻量级的存内调度技术，通过复用现有SSD内部结构来最大化闪存核心的并行度。

Result: GRAINS 在性能上实现了 2.7x-47.8x 的加速（相比软件基准）和 1.5x-17.0x 的加速（相比硬件加速基准）；在能效上实现了 4.4x-31.6x 的降低（相比软件基准）和 3.1x-20.7x 的降低（相比硬件加速基准）。

Conclusion: GRAINS 通过算法与架构的协同设计有效解决了大规模基因组图分析在存储中的性能瓶颈，显著提升了处理速度并降低了能耗，为存储内计算在生物信息学领域的应用提供了极具潜力的解决方案。

Abstract: Graph-based representations of genome sequences have emerged as a powerful approach for representing massive genomic databases in an expressive and efficient way. Despite their benefits, analysis on large-scale genome graphs incurs significant data movement overhead from the storage system due to accessing large amounts of low-reuse data. Processing data directly inside the storage device can be a fundamental solution for mitigating this overhead. However, none of the existing tools for graph-based genome analysis can be efficiently used inside the storage system due to the limited internal hardware resources in modern SSDs. At the same time, prior storage-centric systems developed for (i) traditional, linear non-graph-based genome analysis or (ii) conventional, non-genomic graph analysis are not suitable for the unique data structures and access patterns of graph-based genome analysis.
  We propose GRAINS, the first system for analysis with large-scale genome graphs in storage. Through our detailed examination of typical analysis pipelines that operate on genome graphs, we perform storage-aware algorithm-architecture co-design to (i) make these pipelines more storage-friendly and (ii) further improve performance, energy-efficiency, and cost via in-storage and in-flash processing. GRAINS's co-design is based on three key aspects. First, we propose a new batching and execution flow, based on unique features of genome graphs. Second, via in-flash and in-storage processing, we avoid transferring low-reused flash pages. Third, to leverage the full parallelism of flash dies, we design an effective, yet lightweight, scheduling technique, enabled by re-purposing the existing SSD structures. GRAINS provides 2.7x-47.8x speedup (4.4x-31.6x energy reduction) over the state-of-the-art software baselines, and 1.5x-17.0x speedup (3.1x-20.7x energy reduction) over a hardware-accelerated baseline.

</details>


### [252] [SegFold: Accelerating Sparse GEMM with a Fine-Grained Dynamic Dataflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26701)
*Xinrui Wu,Hanyu Wang,Jason Cong,Tony Nowatzki*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文针对SpGEMM计算中静态数据流的局限性，提出了动态调度架构Segment及加速器SegFold，通过细粒度任务重映射与动态负载平衡，显著提升了稀疏矩阵乘法的计算性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SpGEMM加速方案大多依赖静态数据流（如内积、外积等），在面对多样的稀疏矩阵时，无法兼顾数据复用率与计算负载均衡，导致硬件资源利用效率低下。

Method: 提出了一种名为“Segment”的动态数据流架构，并基于此设计了SegFold加速器。其核心技术包括：1) 内存控制器：在局部窗口内识别细粒度数据复用机会并动态分配工作；2) 归并网络（Merge Network）：在处理单元（PE）间动态重映射计算任务，实现负载均衡。

Result: 在多种矩阵密度和规模下，SegFold相较于最先进的SpGEMM加速器实现了1.95倍的加速，相较于最优静态数据流配置实现了5.3倍的加速。

Conclusion: 通过引入动态数据流调度，打破了传统静态调度在SpGEMM计算中对数据复用和负载均衡的限制，证明了动态调度在提升SpGEMM性能方面的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Generalized sparse matrix-matrix multiplication (SpGEMM) is critical in many domains. Existing CPUs and GPUs, as well as specialized accelerators, rely on static dataflows (e.g., inner product, outer product, Gustavson, etc.). Each static dataflow sacrifices some data reuse opportunities and imposes constraints on load balance.
  To address this inefficiency, we extend the typical SpGEMM dataflows by considering dynamism. Specifically, we add fine-grained dynamic scheduling to optimize reuse and reduce resource contention. We also develop dynamic remapping of partially completed work to improve load balance and parallelism. These ideas are formalized into a specific dataflow called Segment. To demonstrate Segment, we codesign a SpGEMM accelerator called SegFold. SegFold includes a memory controller that identifies fine-grained reuse opportunities in a local window of the stationary input array and exploits them through dynamic work assignment. It also incorporates a merge network that routes inputs to appropriate processing elements (PEs) for computation while dynamically remapping the work assigned to each PE to balance load.
  Across diverse densities and matrix sizes, SegFold achieves a geometric-mean $1.95\times$ speedup over state-of-the-art SpGEMM accelerators and $5.3\times$ over the best static dataflow configuration, demonstrating that adding dynamism to the dataflow design space unlocks reuse and load-balance gains that no static scheduling choice can achieve in isolation.

</details>


### [253] [Residual GPU Cache State on Apple M4 Pro](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27098)
*Faruk Alpay,Baris Basaran*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文对 Apple M4 Pro 的统一内存架构进行了实验测定，发现 GPU 任务会带来显著的共享缓存置换现象，并定量评估了该影响及其软件恢复方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Apple Silicon 采用统一内存架构，但 GPU 完成指令后留下的缓存状态缺乏官方文档说明，影响 CPU 在 GPU 之后的执行性能。

Method: 基于 M4 Pro 芯片，通过对比 GPU 负载与 CPU 探测（STREAM/BabelStream 验证）的延迟，结合 PMU（性能监控单元）和 IOReport 硬件计数器，分析缓存填充模式和内存访问冲突。

Result: 实验发现 GPU 的大工作集（0-512 MiB）会置换共享缓存，导致随后的 CPU 访问变慢。通过第二次 CPU 遍历可证实这是缓存置换而非 DRAM 竞争。此外，在 CPU 高优先级流量下，GPU 受到背景 QoS 影响较小。

Conclusion: 研究明确了 Apple M4 Pro 上 GPU 完成工作后残留的缓存置换效应，并验证了一种简单的单遍软件恢复机制可以有效消除该延迟。

Abstract: Apple silicon exposes unified CPU-GPU memory, but the cache state left after a completed GPU command is not documented. This paper characterizes that phase boundary on a 14-core Apple M4 Pro. We validate the measurement pipeline against unmodified STREAM 5.10 and BabelStream 5.0, then adapt an 8192-byte system-level-cache occupancy pattern to a synchronized Metal experiment. A GPU kernel touches 0 to 512 MiB and finishes before a 16 MiB CPU probe begins. The first CPU traversal is slower after large GPU footprints, while a second traversal removes most of the cost, showing residual shared-cache displacement rather than simultaneous DRAM contention. A separate matched-block experiment measures GPU slowdown under high-priority CPU traffic and finds background QoS close to baseline. Root PMU measurements and public IOReport histograms provide hardware grounding: they distinguish L1D refill sectors from software cache-line size, expose page-offset-dependent conflict behavior, and separate performance-core, efficiency-core, and AGX demand. The results identify a reproducible post-GPU cache-displacement window on M4 Pro and quantify a simple one-pass software recovery mechanism.

</details>


### [254] [Evaluating Architectural Trade-offs in CGRAs: The Impact of Scratchpad Memory and Heterogeneity on Compute-Intensive Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27240)
*María José Belda,Lara Orlandic,Fernando Castro,Miguel Peón-Quirós,Katzalin Olcoz,David Atienza*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文分析了CGRA设计中处理单元异构性和缓存支持对边缘算力的影响，发现同构架构在面积和执行速度上更优，而异构架构在特定数据处理任务上能效更高，为边缘计算架构选型提供了权衡策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代边缘计算（如视觉Transformer）对高吞吐量、空间并行性和能效有严苛要求，亟需探索粗粒度可重构架构（CGRA）中处理单元异构性和局部数据重用设计对算力、面积和能效的影响。

Method: 该研究通过实验评估了两种CGRA配置：基线同构架构与集成专用功能单元及暂存存储器（SPM）的异构架构。测试工作负载涵盖了核心计算内核（FFT、GEMM）及端到端的癫痫检测Transformer任务。

Result: SPM显著优化了数据移动（内存通信减少8倍）。同构设计在面积开销上具有4.4倍至8.2倍的优势，且支持700MHz主频，在矩阵计算中比异构配置快5倍；而异构架构在数据洗牌任务中展现了更高的能效。

Conclusion: 该工作为边缘计算应用选择CGRA架构提供了路线图，强调了在资源受限的环境下，需根据算术强度、性能目标和资源预算，在异构设计的能效优势与同构设计的面积/频率优势之间进行权衡。

Abstract: Modern edge computing applications, particularly high-throughput stream processing like Vision Transformers (ViTs), demand massive spatial parallelism and efficient data movement under tight power and area constraints. Coarse-Grained Reconfigurable Architectures (CGRAs) offer a promising paradigm to balance performance, flexibility, and energy efficiency. This paper analyzes the impact of two critical CGRA design choices: processing element heterogeneity and local data reuse support. We evaluate essential computational kernels (Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) and General Matrix Multiply (GEMM)) alongside an end-to-end seizure detection transformer workload across two distinct configurations: a baseline homogeneous architecture and a heterogeneous evolution integrating specialized functional units with an Scratchpad Memory (SPM). Our evaluation demonstrates that the SPM significantly optimizes data movement, reducing memory traffic eightfold compared to a memory-less design. While the heterogeneous architecture achieves superior energy efficiency for data-shuffling tasks, the homogeneous design minimizes area overhead by 4.4x to 8.2x relative to state-of-the-art CGRAs. Furthermore, it sustains a 700 MHz operating frequency, enabling up to a 5x execution speedup over the heterogeneous configuration during matrix computations. Ultimately, this work provides an architectural roadmap for selecting CGRA fabrics based on the arithmetic intensity, performance goals, and resource envelopes of edge-scale workloads.

</details>


### [255] [CHIA: An open-source framework for principled, agentic AI-driven hardware/software co-design research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27350)
*Angela Cui,Ferran Hermida-Rivera,Jack Toubes,Raghav Gupta,Jim Fang,Chengyi Lux Zhang,Ella Schwarz,Junha Kim,Yakun Sophia Shao,Borivoje Nikolic,Christopher W. Fletcher,Sagar Karandikar*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: CHIA是一个用于软硬件协同设计的开源AI驱动研究框架，通过标准化设计工作流（CHIA loops）和大规模分布式执行支持，显著提升了在计算机架构和系统研究中集成代理AI的效率与科学性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前AI在软硬件协同设计中的应用多局限于小规模、孤立的实验，缺乏一套能够支持复杂、可扩展、标准化的AI驱动研发工作流的框架。

Method: 提出CHIA框架：基于“CHIA loops”（有向循环图）构建自动化设计流程，循环图中的节点集成了Chipyard、gem5、FireSim等主流工具及AI模型；提供了一套支持异构系统大规模部署、容错运行及性能分析的库函数和基础设施。

Result: 通过五个典型案例验证了CHIA的有效性，包括RTL与模拟器对齐、LLM驱动的微架构实现、IPC感知架构优化、进化式架构搜索以及智能体代码修复。

Conclusion: CHIA通过建立标准化和可扩展的框架，有效解决了目前智能体AI在软硬件协同设计领域应用零散、扩展性差的问题，为实现更加自动化和科学化的计算机系统设计提供了基础工具。

Abstract: Agentic artificial intelligence shows great promise for radically improving the pace of innovation in hardware/software co-design research across computer architecture, systems, compilers, and VLSI. Thus far, however, applications of AI in these contexts have generally been demonstrated in isolated settings on small-scale problems, due to the difficulty of designing and deploying complex AI-infused hardware and software development workflows.
  This paper introduces CHIA, an open-source hardware/software co-design framework for agile and principled research on the application of AI to co-design. CHIA treats the productive construction and scalable deployment of the co-design flow itself as a first-class objective. In CHIA, agentic AI-driven hardware and software design flows are expressed as \textit{CHIA loops}: directed cyclic graphs whose nodes execute various system-on-chip design tools, microarchitectural simulators, software build systems, AI models, evolutionary coding agents, and more. The \textit{CHIA library} provides node implementations for many popular tools, including Chipyard, gem5, ChampSim, FireSim, Hammer (thus several commercial ASIC CAD tools), Vivado, AlphaEvolve, AdaEvolve, and many others.
  CHIA also provides a broad set of features to conduct principled science around these flows. These include isolation between AI models and hardware tools, profiling mechanisms, fault-tolerant execution, and reliability at scale across hundreds of heterogeneous systems (CPUs, FPGAs, GPUs, etc., across public cloud/on-prem.).
  To showcase CHIA, we present five CHIA loops as case studies: (1) automatic RTL-to-gem5 simulator alignment, (2) LLM-driven implementation of microarchitectural features in RTL, (3) agentic, IPC-aware critical path optimization, (4) evolutionary architectural discovery, and (5) maintainer-friendly agentic GitHub issue fixing.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [256] [Hot AI in Cold Space: Thermal-Crosstalk-Aware Scheduling for Sustainable Orbital AI Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26150)
*Shuyi Chen,Zhengchang Hua,Nikos Tziritas,Georgios Theodoropoulos*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 为解决轨道数据中心因高密度部署导致的热瓶颈与硬件加速老化问题，论文提出了“热感知负载均衡（TLB）”方案，通过动态迁移工作负载至低温节点，平衡AI训练性能与硬件使用寿命，实现轨道AI的可持续化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面AI训练面临严重的能源和水资源危机，而将数据中心部署在轨道上（ODCs）虽可实现“零运行碳排放”，但受限于分布式LLM训练对低延迟的要求，导致极高的物理密度，进而引发“邻近-热悖论”（热-流体串扰和热-辐射串扰导致散热失效）。如果不加以解决，散热不足将导致热节流并加速硬件疲劳和空间电子垃圾的产生。

Method: 提出“热感知异构假设”，并开发了热负载均衡（TLB）软件框架。该框架能够根据瞬时流体温度或辐射热吸收量，将LLM工作负载动态迁移至温度较低的计算单元。

Result: TLB策略能够有效解决热瓶颈，恢复模型算力利用率（MFU），同时减轻硬件的热应力，延长设备寿命，从而摊薄发射成本并减少电子垃圾。

Conclusion: 轨道数据中心（ODCs）若要实现真正的可持续性，必须通过热感知负载均衡（TLB）策略，将热管理纳入资源调度，而非仅依赖简单的负载均匀分配，从而在大规模AI训练中平衡算力性能与硬件寿命。

Abstract: Terrestrial AI training faces an unsustainable energy and water crisis, positioning Orbital Data Centers (ODCs) as a "zero operational carbon" alternative. However, the sub-$10μ\text{s}$ communication latency required for distributed Large Language Model (LLM) training forces ODCs into extreme physical density, triggering a critical "Proximity-Thermal Paradox." As these high-density systems scale into Monolithic Structures or Proximity Swarms, they suffer from intense thermal-fluid crosstalk (heat traps in shared cooling loops) and thermal-radiative crosstalk (mutual heating that blocks deep-space cooling radiators). If left unmitigated, this persistent heat stagnation not only triggers severe thermal throttling that degrades training throughput, but also induces severe thermal fatigue, drastically shortening hardware lifespans and generating premature space e-waste. To make orbital AI truly sustainable, this position paper challenges traditional uniform load-sharing. We propose the Thermal-Aware Heterogeneity Thesis, which treats spatial cooling variances as a primary resource management dimension. Building on this, we introduce Thermal-Load Balancing (TLB), a software framework that dynamically migrates LLM workloads to the coolest available units based on instantaneous fluid temperatures or absorbed radiation. Our analysis demonstrates that TLB resolves thermal bottlenecks to restore Model Flops Utilization (MFU), while simultaneously reducing physical thermal stress. Extending the operational lifespan of orbital hardware is crucial to amortize the massive embodied carbon of rocket launches, outlining a necessary pathway to scale orbital AI without accelerating e-waste.

</details>


### [257] [FinWhale: An Optimally Resilient Two-Round Terminating DAG Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26292)
*Razya Ladelsky,Roy Friedman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FinWhale 是首个支持两消息延迟快速路径的 DAG 类 BFT 协议，通过在 Mysticeti 协议中引入高效的提交机制，成功实现了高性能与低延迟的结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于 DAG 的拜占庭容错（BFT）协议在高吞吐量下表现良好，但在最优环境下的提交延迟通常至少需要三个消息延迟。现有的快速路径 BFT 协议虽然能实现最优的两个消息延迟，但难以自然扩展至 DAG。因此，作者旨在设计一种既能保持 DAG 高吞吐优势，又能实现两消息延迟快速路径的协议。

Method: FinWhale 在 Mysticeti 协议基础上，引入了一种新型的基于“快速路径证据块（fast path evidence blocks）”的快速路径提交机制，该机制能够与原有的慢速路径规则安全共存，并引入了新的提交结构以保障不同本地 DAG 视图之间的一致性。

Result: FinWhale 在部分同步模型下运作，满足了快速拜占庭共识的已知下界（n=3f+2p-1），能够容忍多达 f 个拜占庭故障，并在快速路径中最多有 p（1≤p≤f）个验证者失败时实现快速终止。

Conclusion: FinWhale 证明了在无证书 DAG 共识协议中集成最优延迟的快速路径是可行的，通过结合快速路径和慢速路径推理，在保持安全性的前提下实现了性能优化。

Abstract: DAG based Byzantine Fault Tolerant protocols provide high throughput consensus under partial synchrony but existing DAG protocols still require at least three message delays to commit decisions. In contrast fast path Byzantine Fault Tolerant protocols can achieve optimal two message delay termination under favorable conditions though they do not naturally extend to DAGs.
  We present FinWhale the first DAG based Byzantine Fault Tolerant protocol with a two message delay fast path. FinWhale extends Mysticeti with a novel fast path commit mechanism that safely coexists with the protocol's original slow path rules. To preserve safety across different local DAG views we introduce new commit structures based on fast path evidence blocks enabling validators to combine fast path and slow path reasoning consistently.
  FinWhale operates in the partially synchronous model with n equals three f plus two p minus one validators matching the known lower bound for fast Byzantine consensus. The protocol tolerates up to f Byzantine faults and achieves fast termination whenever at most p validators fail during the fast path where p is between one and f. Our results show that optimal latency fast paths can be integrated into uncertified DAG consensus protocols.

</details>


### [258] [A Distributed Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm Simulator for Engineering Design Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26297)
*Ali Rajabi,Milad Hasanzadeh,Amin Kargarian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一款基于Qiskit的开源分布式QAOA模拟器，支持单体与分布式量子计算模式，通过多种运行时优化技术显著提升了QUBO问题的求解效率，并在实际工程案例中验证了其准确性与实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决现有的QUBO问题求解工具在不同QAOA执行模式下缺乏通用性、可重用性和跨QPU扩展性的问题，以支持工程设计和决策应用中复杂优化问题的求解。

Method: 开发了一个兼容Qiskit的分布式QAOA（DQAOA）模拟器，支持单体和分布式两种模式；流程包括QUBO模型标准化、哈密顿量映射、变量分配、线路构建，并辅以参数化电路复用、目标重复使用、分批评估及并行多启动等优化技术；提供基于Streamlit的GUI界面。

Result: 通过在QUBO基准测试和电力机组组合场景中的验证，模拟器结果与经典QAOA完全一致；运行时分析证明了所采用的优化策略能显著减少计算开销，能够成功在分布式环境下处理复杂的优化任务。

Conclusion: 该模拟器在处理QUBO问题时保持了与经典单体QAOA一致的结果准确性。尽管分布式QAOA由于涉及跨QPU通信而存在性能开销，但通过参数化电路复用、目标重用和并行计算等运行时优化技术，能有效降低总体计算负担，为工程设计与决策应用中的大规模QUBO问题求解提供了可扩展的工具。

Abstract: This paper presents a Qiskit-compatible distributed quantum approximate optimization algorithm (DQAOA) simulator for quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) problems arising in engineering design and decision applications. The open-source simulator is available through the RAISE LAB website and GitHub repository, with README documentation for installation, input formatting, configurable parameters, and example workflows. The package addresses the need for a reusable simulator that can solve and compare QUBO instances across different QAOA execution modes. It supports monolithic QAOA on a single quantum processing unit (QPU) and distributed QAOA across a user-specified number of QPUs with configurable capacities. The workflow canonicalizes the QUBO model, maps it to a cost Hamiltonian, allocates variables across QPUs, identifies local and cross-QPU couplings, and constructs the corresponding circuits. Runtime optimizations, including parameterized circuit reuse, objective reuse at fixed depth, batched evaluations, and parallel multi-start execution, reduce repeated overhead. A Streamlit graphical user interface is also provided for entering or uploading QUBO instances, configuring solver settings, running selected modes, and visualizing solution-quality metrics without editing Python scripts. The package is demonstrated on standalone QUBO benchmarks and a power generation unit commitment application. In the unit commitment case, brute force, monolithic QAOA, and distributed QAOA recover the same commitment bitstring and operating cost. Across multiple case studies, the simulator produces results consistent with classical monolithic QAOA references in terms of optimal bitstrings and costs. Staged runtime analysis shows substantial runtime reduction across implementation stages, while distributed QAOA remains more demanding because cross-QPU couplings require remote operations.

</details>


### [259] [Priceless: An examination of Serverless Functions-as-a-Service (FaaS) pricing models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26308)
*Nnamdi Ekwe-Ekwe*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Priceless框架，深入测评了主流云厂商（AWS、Azure、GCP）的FaaS定价模型，发现不同厂商及区域间存在显著成本差异，其中AWS性价比最高，Azure最贵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着服务商功能的扩展，FaaS的定价模型变得日益复杂和多样化，用户难以直观判断成本差异，因此需要对当前FaaS生态的定价现状进行深入分析。

Method: 对现有主流FaaS提供商（AWS Lambda、Microsoft Azure Functions、Google Cloud Functions）的功能及定价模型进行全面调研，并基于典型工作负载进行对比成本分析。

Result: 揭示了当前FaaS市场存在显著的跨提供商及跨区域价格差异；通过对比发现，AWS在成本效率上具有优势，Azure则相对较高。

Conclusion: AWS是运行FaaS工作负载成本最低的平台，而微软Azure在同等工作负载下的成本最高。此外，跨云服务商和跨单服务商区域之间的定价差异显著。

Abstract: Serverless Functions-as-a-Service providers have grown in their offering since inception a decade ago, with a myriad of new functionalities offered to end-users. These new features have also brought new, varied and at times complex pricing models that differ between providers. In this paper, we present Priceless, a detailed examination of the current state of the art of FaaS features, and their pricing models. We then perform a comparative price analysis of running example workloads across AWS Lambda, Microsoft Azure Functions and Google Cloud Functions. Our work finds significant cost differences both cross-provider, but also, cross-region within the provider. We find that AWS is the cheapest overall to run functions on, with Microsoft Azure being the most expensive for equivalent workloads.

</details>


### [260] [Moebius: Serving Mixture-of-Expert Models with Seamless Runtime Parallelism Switch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26607)
*Shaoyu Wang,Yizhuo Liang,Jaeyong Song,Chong Li,Seo Jin Park*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Moebius 是一款为 MoE 模型设计的运行时自适应推理系统，它能根据并发负载动态切换专家并行（EP）和张量并行（TP），消除了因静态部署策略在负载波动时带来的性能牺牲。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 模型在不同并发场景下（如高并发的 EP 优势 vs 低并发的 TP 优势）性能表现不同，但现有的系统通常采用静态布局，无法适应生产环境中持续波动的负载需求，导致算力浪费或延迟增加。

Method: 提出 Moebius 系统，通过在内存中同时保留 EP 和 TP 两种布局，利用高速 GPU 互联和融合的 GPU-to-GPU 传输内核，在不中断服务的情况下实现对专家权重和 KV Cache 的实时重分片切换。

Result: 在 Qwen3-235B-A22B 模型上，Moebius 在所有工作点均达到或超过了最优静态并行性能；在 RL 推理场景中比静态策略提升 1.16–1.25 倍；单次切换耗时仅 215–434 ms，内存额外开销仅 2.4%。

Conclusion: Moebius 通过在运行时动态切换 EP 和 TP 布局，打破了 MoE 模型在推理中受限于静态并行策略的性能瓶颈，实现了在不同并发负载下的最优性能表现，且切换开销极低。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale large language models (LLMs) to hundreds of billions of parameters. Serving a single MoE model requires multiple GPUs operating in parallel, typically through tensor parallelism (TP) or expert parallelism (EP). The optimal choice depends on the number of in-flight requests: TP is faster at low concurrency, whereas EP wins at high concurrency. Production workloads cross this boundary continually: online serving sees bursty arrivals that subside into quiet periods, and reinforcement-learning rollouts begin as a high-concurrency burst that decays into a long tail of stragglers. Pinning either layout therefore forfeits performance when the workload crosses to the other side.
  We present Moebius, a serving system that switches between EP and TP at runtime without restarting the engine or dropping in-flight requests. Our key insight is that EP and TP are two layouts of one model, not two models: they compute the same function over byte-identical expert weights and KV cache, so a switch changes only which rank owns each slice. Moving those owner-changed slices is the sole irreducible cost, and modern high-bandwidth GPU interconnects make it fast enough to do between decode steps without draining in-flight requests. Moebius preserves each parallelism's runtime resident, and reshards the single copy of expert weights and KV cache at fixed addresses with fused GPU-to-GPU transfer kernels. On 8x H200 GPUs serving Qwen3-235B-A22B, Moebius matches the better static parallelism at every operating point, and beats it on RL rollouts by 1.16-1.25x across steps. Each switch completes in 215-434 ms, and Moebius holds both layouts resident with only 2.4% memory overhead.

</details>


### [261] [Simulating Unified Tensor Resharding in heterogeneous AI systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26633)
*Sumit Kumar,Sayantan Dasgupta,Kushal Mitra,Meet Dadhania,Rohan Sudhir Basugade,Praveen Tammana,Satananda Burla,Abed Mohammad Kamaluddin,Rinku Shah*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Xsim是首个专为异构计算环境设计的分布式LLM训练模拟器，通过异构感知通信、负载均衡及灵活并行抽象，实现了高精度的训练时间预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI训练模拟器主要针对同构计算环境设计，而现实场景中模型架构（如MoE）、公有云硬件供应限制及地理分布式的企业基础设施均呈现出明显的异构性，缺乏能够精准模拟这些异构环境的工具。

Method: 构建了一个名为Xsim的异构感知分布式LLM训练模拟器，核心技术包括：(1) 支持不均匀负载均衡的任务划分；(2) 异构感知的集合通信环构建与块划分；(3) 可重用的流水线并行与张量重分片抽象；(4) 灵活的部署计划输入抽象；(5) 与NS-3和htsim的插件式集成以平衡模拟保真度与性能。

Result: 实验表明，Xsim在大多数异构数据并行/张量并行配置下的预测误差小于5%，在流水线并行通信建模中达到了约2%的误差，且能有效提供流水线气泡时间和滞后任务等待时间等关键指标。

Conclusion: Xsim通过引入异构感知机制，成功解决了现有训练模拟器在异构基础设施环境下预测精度不足的问题，为复杂分布式LLM训练场景提供了高效、准确的分析工具。

Abstract: State-of-the-art AI training simulators assume homogeneous compute and network infrastructure. However, real-world training infrastructure is becoming increasingly heterogeneous since: (a) Model architectures such as multimodal and MoE exploit heterogeneity to improve device utilization, (b) Public cloud platforms often provide limited availability of homogeneous hardware due to fast hardware evolution, and (c) Large enterprises frequently deploy geographically distributed infrastructure that is both diverse and heterogeneous. In this paper, we present Xsim, a heterogeneity-aware simulator for distributed LLM training. Xsim supports: (i) Load balancing through non-uniform workload partitioning across heterogeneous device groups, (ii) Heterogeneity-aware collective communication via customized ring construction and chunk partitioning, (iii) Reusable heterogeneity-aware abstractions for emerging pipeline-parallel algorithms and non-uniform tensor resharding technique, (iv) Flexible input abstractions for specifying deployment plans with custom device groups and custom device-to-parallelism mappings, and (v) Pluggable integration with NS-3 and htsim, allowing users to trade off simulation fidelity for performance and scalability. Our evaluation demonstrates that Xsim accurately predicts training time for real-world heterogeneous deployments, with an error of less than 5% across most heterogeneous data-parallel/tensor-parallel configurations and around 2% error with pipeline-parallel communication modeling. We expose actionable metrics such as pipeline bubble time and straggler waiting time.

</details>


### [262] [RolloutPipe: Overlapping Pipelined Rollout and Training in Disaggregated On-Policy LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26997)
*Rongjian Chen,Jianmin Hu,Kejiang Ye,Minxian Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RolloutPipe是一种用于分布式RLVR系统的高效训练框架，通过流水线化滚动任务与优化调度，在不牺牲同策略正确性的前提下，极大缓解了训练器的等待浪费问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有解耦RLVR系统（如Slime）在处理同步同策略GRPO时，因Rollout与训练两个阶段等待时间不匹配导致的训练器空闲（GPU利用率低）或异步导致的策略偏移（训练 stale 数据）的问题。

Method: 提出了RolloutPipe框架，包含：①完全组流水线（CGP）：打破了必须等待整个批次滚动完成的限制，一旦最小可训练组完成即发送至训练器；②前沿组调度（FGD）：优化滚动节点任务调度，优先处理形成训练批次所需的前沿样本，确保训练器资源被更稳定地利用。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B模型及多个推理与科学基准测试中，RolloutPipe将滚动到训练完成的总耗时缩短了30.7%-42.3%，并将训练器等待比率降低了37%-76%。

Conclusion: RolloutPipe通过CGP和FGD技术解决了解耦RLVR架构中Rollout与Trainer之间的数据饥饿问题，在保持同策略（on-policy）准确性的前提下，显著降低了训练器等待时间，提升了系统整体效能。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) post-training for reasoning increasingly relies on reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), where models learn from ground-truth feedback on mathematical, logical, and scientific tasks. To enable flexible resource allocation and support heterogeneous training setups, modern RLVR systems adopt disaggregated architectures that decouple rollout generation and policy training across independent GPU pools. However, existing synchronous on-policy GRPO (Group Relative Policy Optimization) RLVR systems finish an entire rollout before starting training, leaving the trainer GPU pool idle while rollout is still ongoing. Asynchronous RL pipelines overlap the two stages, but at the cost of training on stale data. To address these challenges, we propose RolloutPipe, a post-training framework for disaggregated RLVR systems, which turns the fixed-weight rollout into a complete-group pipeline where trainable groups move to the trainer while later groups are still being generated. RolloutPipe achieves this through two techniques including complete-group pipelining (CGP) and frontier-group dispatch (FGD). CGP dispatches each trainable complete group to the trainer FIFO as soon as group materialization finishes, and FGD is an admission policy on the Rollout node that first admits requests for the frontier groups needed to form the next training batch, so that trainer-ready groups arrive earlier and more steadily. The design starts training before the rollout completes while maintaining on-policy correctness. Evaluated on Qwen3-1.7B across four reasoning and science benchmarks and twelve rollout settings, RolloutPipe shortens the rollout-to-train-end time by 30.7%-42.3%, and lowers the trainer waiting ratio by 37%-76% compared to Slime, a state-of-the-art rollout and training system.

</details>


### [263] [DMuon: Efficient Distributed Muon Training with Near-Adam Overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27153)
*Vincent Chen,Starrick Liu,Regis Cheng,Dance Yang,Shalfun Li,Ryan Yu,Lucy Liang,Hang Su,Roy Gan,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DMuon通过高性能的分布式实现优化了Muon优化器的计算开销，使其在保持高性能收敛的同时，在分布式训练中实现了接近AdamW的训练延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon等矩阵正交化优化器在收敛性上优势显著，但现有的分布式训练基础设施是为元素级优化器设计的，导致Muon在实现时存在高额的计算开销（超过前向/反向传播的两倍），亟需一种高效的分布式实现方案。

Method: 提出了DMuon，这是一种针对Muon优化器的分布式实现，通过优化Newton-Schulz迭代及分布式架构设计，使其作为即插即用模块接入现有训练流水线，无需修改底层框架。

Result: DMuon在具身智能基础模型和LLM训练中表现出色，端到端单步时间提速1.48倍至3.01倍，优化器步时间提速6.85倍至163.00倍，将延迟降低至接近AdamW的水平。

Conclusion: DMuon 通过高效的分布式实现，成功解决了矩阵正交化优化器（如Muon）在分布式训练中的高开销问题，使其性能达到了与AdamW相当的水平，促进了其在超大规模模型中的落地。

Abstract: Matrix-orthogonalization-based optimizers, exemplified by Muon, have demonstrated strong convergence behavior across a wide range of modern deep learning workloads. The matrix-aware updates offer a compelling alternative to conventional element-wise optimization, particularly as model architectures continue to grow in scale and heterogeneity. Yet contemporary distributed training infrastructure built around the assumption of element-wise optimizers is poorly matched to matrix-level optimizers such as Muon, whose updates couple entire weight matrices and require costly Newton-Schulz iterations. Vanilla Muon implementations incur more than 2x the cost of forward and backward passes. To close this gap, we present DMuon, an open-source distributed Muon implementation that integrates into existing training pipelines as a drop-in module, with no framework-level modifications. Across both embodied foundation model and large language model (LLM) training workloads, DMuon achieves a 1.48x-3.01x speedup in end-to-end step time and a 6.85x-163.00x speedup in optimizer-step time, bringing per-step latency to near-AdamW levels and enabling efficient scaling in our model training.

</details>


### [264] [CHAMB-GA: A Containerized HPC Scalable Microservice-Based Framework for Genetic Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27217)
*Felix Bonhoff,Thiemo Pesch,Andrea Benigni,Alexander Mitsos,Manuel Dahmen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CHAMB-GA 是一种基于容器化微服务的遗传算法框架，通过解耦遗传算子与仿真评估，实现了在云平台（Kubernetes）与 HPC 集群（SLURM）间的高性能、可扩展与无缝切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的元启发式优化工具链存在刚性强、硬件绑定等问题，难以在从本地到大规模分布式 HPC 集群的跨度上进行高效扩展和复杂仿真集成，导致计算资源利用率低。

Method: 提出了一种基于容器化微服务的框架，利用消息代理实现异步管理，将遗传算法算子与仿真后端解耦，并通过容器编排（Kubernetes）和批处理调度（SLURM）实现跨平台的分布式并行扩展。

Result: 在基准测试中实现了超过 3,500 个 CPU 核心的线性扩展，证明了极低的性能开销；在 HVDC 线路调度优化实际案例中，验证了其在不同基础设施间的平稳迁移能力及多阶段工作流集成的有效性。

Conclusion: CHAMB-GA 提供了一个可扩展、可移植且易于集成外部工具的微服务架构，成功实现了从个人电脑到 HPC 集群和云平台的无缝迁移，为计算密集型的元启发式优化提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Metaheuristic-based global optimization with embedded, long-running simulations is a computationally expensive process. To support various stages of development and execution, a seamless transition from personal computers to distributed clusters is desired, enabling execution across all computational scales. However, existing tool chains are often characterized by rigidity and hardware-bound constraints, which impede scalability and the integration of complex simulations. Bridging this gap, we present a containerized HPC scalable microservice-based framework for genetic algorithms with embedded simulations (CHAMB-GA). The deployment of the framework scales consistently across cloud infrastructure via container orchestration and HPC clusters via batch-scheduled parallel execution. Users provide the GA operators and simulation backend separately. The framework is designed to run these components in a distributed and decoupled manner, mapped to separate hardware. This approach ensures that the fitness evaluation and genetic operations are not managed within the same process and are utilizing distinct parts of the compute infrastructure. A central message broker coordinates asynchronous manager-worker communication between microservices, thereby parallelizing evolutionary operations and fitness evaluations. We demonstrate CHAMB-GA's scalability, portability, and reproducibility, while facilitating the integration of external tools and complex simulations on benchmark and powerflow problems. The capabilities of CHAMB-GA are validated in a two-part approach: (i) a benchmark study demonstrating minimal overhead while scaling to over 3,500 CPU cores, and (ii) a dispatch optimization of High Voltage Direct Current (HVDC) lines in the German transmission grid, showing seamless migration from Kubernetes to SLURM, combined horizontal and vertical scaling, and integration of multi-stage workflows.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [265] [Detecting and Controlling Sycophancy with Cascading Linear Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26155)
*Maty Bohacek,Rishub Jain,Nicholas Dufour,Thomas Leung,Chris Bregler,Roma Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过迭代生成级联线性样本，不仅提升了对LLM谄媚行为的特征解耦能力，还实现了更高效、更具可解释性的模型行为检测与自动化转向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型激活转向（activation steering）方法高度依赖人工构建的二元对比样本，且难以有效解耦模型内部复杂的行为特征，限制了对模型行为（如“谄媚”倾向）的准确控制。

Method: 提出了一种迭代数据生成流水线，通过“级联样本（cascading samples）”获取呈现出与行为线性相关特征程度的数据，利用这些样本识别并隔离级联线性特征，进而构建用于行为检测与转向的线性可分空间。

Result: 实验表明，通过级联样本发现的特征能够形成线性可分空间，在检测谄媚行为、确定得分及实现鲁棒转向方面，表现优于“LLM-as-a-judge”和系统提示词（system prompting）基线方法，同时降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: 级联样本生成方法优于传统的二元对比样本，通过隔离可线性扩展的特征，实现了对模型行为（如谄媚行为）更精确的解耦、检测与转向，且具备更高的计算效率和可解释性。

Abstract: Interpreting and controlling model behaviors through activation steering methods requires many pairs of contrastive samples that clearly exhibit desired or undesired behavior. These data pairs determine the degree to which interpretability frameworks can reliably detect model features responsible for a behavior, and therefore the ability to steer models toward or away from such behavior. In this work, we present an iterative data generation pipeline that isolates cascading linear features responsible for a behavior. Specifically, we show how moving beyond simple binary pairs of samples, and instead isolating samples that show degrees of features that scale linearly with behavior, allows for better disentanglement of features. We focus on detecting and steering away from sycophancy -- the tendency of language models to prioritize user validation. We demonstrate that sycophancy features discovered through cascading samples form linearly separable subspaces, and allow for selection of model activations that more clearly correspond to the desired behavior than baseline approaches. We also evaluate their ability to enable detection, deterministic scoring, and robust steering, and see that they either match or outperform LLM-as-a-judge and system prompting baselines while providing lower computational demand and more interpretability guarantees. Code & Data: https://cascading-feats.github.io/

</details>


### [266] [Life After Benchmark Saturation: A Case Study of CORE-Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26158)
*Nitya Nadgir,Sayash Kapoor,Kangheng Liu,Peter Kirgis,Matilda Orona,Stephan Rabanser,Tilman Bayer,Abhishek Shetty,Yue Ling,Derrick Chan-Sew,Rumi Nakagawa,Saiteja Utpala,Zachary S. Siegel,Arvind Narayanan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出当前大模型评估过度依赖准确率，提出了一个包含效率、鲁棒性、人机协作等七个维度的综合评估框架，证明了这些维度在即便准确率饱和后仍能提供有价值的性能洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 AI 评估过度依赖“准确率（Accuracy）”指标，导致当准确率饱和时，既有基准因缺乏对其他核心维度（如鲁棒性、效率、人机协同等）的考量而失去评估价值。

Method: 1. 采用多维度评估框架（构建效度、OOD泛化、效率、可靠性、模型vs支架贡献、人机协作）；2. 以科学代码重现性基准 CORE-Bench 为案例，推出升级版 CORE-Bench v1.1 及 OOD 任务集；3. 通过随机对照实验评估人类与智能体协作的效率提升。

Result: 1. 揭示了现有基准中难以被发现的构建效度威胁；2. 证实了即使在准确率饱和的情况下，测量效率、可靠性及组件贡献度仍具有显著价值；3. 实验显示人机协作可带来约两倍的效率提升。

Conclusion: 单纯追求准确率的评估范式存在局限性。通过考量构建效度、泛化性、效率、可靠性等维度，可以更全面地评估智能体在准确率饱和后的性能，且人机协作能显著提升生产力。

Abstract: When a benchmark's accuracy saturates, it is often retired and replaced with a more challenging version. We show that this approach privileges accuracy and misses the opportunity to study six other key dimensions of agent performance: construct validity issues such as shortcuts, out-of-distribution generalizability, efficiency, reliability, the relative importance of the model versus the scaffold, and uplift from human-agent collaboration. We use CORE-Bench Hard, a benchmark for computational reproducibility of scientific code, as a case study to demonstrate that measuring agents along these dimensions yields meaningful insights into agent performance even after accuracy saturates. First, we surface threats to construct validity in CORE-Bench Hard that are difficult to anticipate with less capable agents. We introduce an improved benchmark, CORE-Bench v1.1, and an out-of-distribution task suite, CORE-Bench OOD. Second, we find that despite accuracy saturation, CORE-Bench v1.1 remains useful for measuring efficiency, reliability, model performance, and scaffold performance. Finally, we conduct a small-scale randomized experiment to measure uplift from human-agent collaboration on real-world computational reproducibility tasks. We find a statistically significant speedup by about a factor of two -- likely underestimated due to one-fifth of human-only reproductions reaching the time limit before completing -- and describe various other findings. Together, our contributions present a more rigorous alternative to the dominant accuracy-centric evaluation paradigm.

</details>


### [267] [Refusal Lives Downstream of Persona in Chat Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26161)
*Viola Zhong,Qirui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究发现模型是否存在拒绝行为受到人格设定的门控影响，拒绝并非孤立机制，而是在角色设定后的深层处理阶段被触发的。通过调整人格方向可直接控制模型的拒绝率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往研究将“拒绝指令（refusal）”和“设定角色（persona）”视为指令微调模型中两个独立的方向，但作者怀疑两者之间存在相互作用，旨在揭示人格设定如何影响模型的拒绝行为。

Method: 1. 提取方向：在 Qwen2.5-7B 和 Llama-3.1-8B 中定位并提取“顺从人格方向”和“拒绝方向”。
2. 激活干预：通过在模型激活空间中对这些方向进行干预（抑制或增强）来观察拒绝率的变化。
3. 层次分析：在不同层级进行投影干预（projection），分析拒绝行为在模型内部处理的层级结构。

Result: 1. 顺从性人格干预能显著抑制模型拒绝行为（在 Llama 中拒绝率从 97% 降至 2%）。
2. 在深层对拒绝方向进行干预能部分恢复拒绝行为，但在浅层则无效。
3. 对深层进行人格方向的投影处理会将模型行为恢复至基线，证明拒绝是在模型处理流的下游被“门控”的。

Conclusion: 拒绝行为（refusal）并非相互独立的机制，而是受角色扮演（persona）控制的。该行为在模型深层表达阶段被门控（gated），表明“拒绝”的激活是在角色设定之后被触发的。因此，研究模型行为时不能孤立看待拒绝机制，必须考虑其与模型人格表现的依赖关系。

Abstract: Linear directions in activation space have been identified for both refusal and persona traits in instruction-tuned chat models, but the two have been studied as separate mechanisms. We show they interact: a compliant persona gates refusal. In Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, we extract a compliant model-persona direction and a refusal direction and intervene on both. Compliant persona steering suppresses refusal -- in Llama, the refusal rate falls from 97% to 2%. Reintroducing the refusal direction partially restores refusal at late layers but not at early ones. Projecting out the persona direction in a late-layer window restores it to baseline; projecting out a random direction does not. Refusal is therefore gated at the late-layer expression stage, downstream of where it is computed. Treating refusal as a single isolated direction misses its dependence on persona.

</details>


### [268] [AlgoEvolve: LLM-driven Meta-evolution of Algorithmic Trading Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26173)
*Dhruv Sharma,Gautam Shroff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AlgoEvolve，一种利用LLM进化算法在算法交易等复杂且不稳定的环境中自动生成并优化交易策略的框架，并通过元进化机制实现了对提示词的自我改进，效果优于人工设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM进化程序研究多集中于静态编码基准测试，缺乏对高噪声、非平稳、高度不连续等复杂动态环境（如算法交易）的应对能力。

Method: 提出了AlgoEvolve框架，利用LLM作为语义变异算子进行程序迭代优化；引入了评估交易策略执行效果的严谨测试协议；并设计了一个元进化（meta-evolutionary）外循环，用于自动优化引导程序合成的提示词（prompts）。

Result: 系统成功实现了具备“政权适应性（regime-adaptive）”的交易逻辑，能够自主调整交易规则；元进化循环发现了优于人工设计的高效搜索启发式策略，降低了零交易故障率，并有效平衡了探索与利用。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的语义进化框架（AlgoEvolve）为在嘈杂、非平稳的复杂环境（如算法交易）中进行持续程序合成提供了切实可行的路径，且通过元进化机制可显著优于人工设计的启发式规则。

Abstract: Recent work shows that Large Language Models (LLMs) can act as semantic mutation operators for the evolutionary discovery of programs and proofs. Most current applications focus on static coding benchmarks. We extend this paradigm to algorithmic trading. This domain is uniquely challenging because it is noisy, non-stationary, and highly discontinuous. We present AlgoEvolve, an LLM-driven evolutionary framework that generates, evaluates, and iteratively improves executable trading strategies. These strategies are expressed as Python code and evaluated through a rigorous testing protocol. Across multiple experiments, the system exhibits emergent regime-adaptive strategy logic, including autonomous shifts in trading rules. We further introduce a meta-evolutionary outer loop that evolves the prompts guiding program synthesis in the inner loop. This outer loop discovers improved search heuristics. These heuristics balance exploration and exploitation while reducing zero-trade failures. They consistently outperform initial human-designed instructions. The results demonstrate that LLM-based semantic evolution provides a viable approach for continual program synthesis in complex environments.

</details>


### [269] [Agentic Analysis for Agentic Infrastructure: An LLM-Powered Pipeline for Comparative Governance of DAO and Corporate AI Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26203)
*Yutian Wang,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文引入了一套基于LLM的大规模治理话语分析框架，通过对比去中心化与企业主导的AI代理标准，揭示了治理结构对技术规范制定过程中的权力分配、参与公平性及议程设置的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理协议的激增，针对其互操作性标准的治理结构缺乏实证研究，亟需一套量化方法来分析治理结构如何影响AI代理生态的社会技术权力格局。

Method: 提出了一套端到端的LLM驱动对比分析流水线，整合了自动化标注（LLM-assisted coding）、神经主题建模（Neural Topic Modeling）和多层网络分析（Multi-layer Network Analysis），并对4,323条治理参与记录进行了实证研究。

Result: 对比分析了ERC-8004（去中心化）与Google A2A（企业主导）两个互操作性标准，发现治理规则显著影响讨论重点；尽管两者存在差异，但在参与不平等和社区碎片化程度上具有可比性；此外，去中心化治理模式能促进更高的话语趋同度。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，尽管治理形式影响实质性焦点，但无论是去中心化还是中心化标准，均表现出相似的参与不平等和社区碎片化。去中心化治理（如ERC-8004）虽然更分散，但能在话语上实现更高的一致性，证明基于LLM的方法论能够有效解析复杂的社会技术治理结构，为制定更公平的AI标准提供实证支持。

Abstract: As AI agent protocols proliferate, the governance structures shaping their interoperability standards remain empirically underexamined. We introduce an LLM-powered comparative pipeline for large-scale governance discourse analysis, integrating automated annotation, neural topic modeling, and multi-layer network analysis to study socio-technical power structures at scale. We validate it on two contrasting standards for agent interoperability: ERC-8004 (permissionless, on-chain) and Google A2A (corporate-led). Analyzing 4,323 governance participation records, we combine LLM-assisted coding, topic modeling, and multi-layer network analysis to examine how institutional design shapes thematic priorities and community structure. We find that while governance form influences substantive focus, both regimes exhibit comparable levels of participation inequality and community fragmentation. Discourse alignment is denser in the permissionless setting, suggesting that open governance may foster greater thematic convergence despite decentralized participation. These findings illustrate how LLM-assisted methods can advance the empirical study of technology governance, with implications for designing more equitable agentic AI standards. All data and code are openly available.

</details>


### [270] [Knowledge-augmented Agentic AI for Mental Health Medication Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26205)
*Huizi Yu,Jian Liu,Wenkong Wang,Lingyao Li,Jiayan Zhou,Zhaoqian Xue,Xiang Li,Xinxin Lin,Zhiying Liang,Zhuoru Wu,Siyuan Ma,Xin Ma,Lizhou Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种整合真实世界患者叙述与官方临床监管数据的知识图谱框架，通过溯源机制将“经验”与“权威事实”区分开，旨在提供更早、更客观的精神科药物安全性预警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精神科药物的安全性信息通常分裂为权威但抽象的监管记录和直观但未经证实的患者叙述。若信息整合不当，可能导致恐惧、反安慰剂效应及用药依从性降低，因此需要一种既能整合信息又不混淆证据与轶事的方法。

Method: 构建了一个基于知识图谱的多智能体框架，整合了466,525条Reddit帖子、60,782条WebMD评论以及20年的FDA不良事件报告系统（FAERS）数据；利用LLM进行实体识别（Medication/Condition），并利用ATC-N、ICD-10和MedDRA词汇表进行标准化和溯源管理。

Result: 实体识别模型性能优异（F1分数高达0.969-0.973）；社交媒体平台间的协同性高，但与监管数据存在差异，显示患者数据具有独立的安全性信号价值。分析发现，社交媒体报告不良事件的时间显著早于FDA记录（以舍曲林为例）。

Conclusion: 研究表明，基于溯源（provenance-aware）的知识图谱集成方法是构建可审计、更具信息量且有助于患者理解精神科药物安全性信息的可行途径，需进一步进行前瞻性验证。

Abstract: Patients increasingly seek medication information online, yet safety knowledge for psychiatric drugs is split between regulatory adverse-event records, which are authoritative but abstract, and patient narratives, which are experience-near but unvalidated. Integrating them without conflating evidence and anecdote is especially consequential in psychiatry, where poorly contextualised information can amplify fear, nocebo responses, and non-adherence. Here we develop a provenance-aware, knowledge-graph-based multi-agent framework unifying 466,525 Reddit posts, 60,782 WebMD reviews, and twenty years of U.S. FDA Adverse Event Reporting System records for nine antidepressants. A large-language-model entity-recognition pipeline benchmarked against physician annotations reached highest F1 scores of 0.969 for medications and 0.973 for conditions. The two community platforms were far more concordant with each other (overlap up to a Jaccard similarity of 0.905) than with regulatory reports, indicating that patient-generated data form a partly independent safety signal. For sertraline, many adverse events appeared in community sources hundreds of days before the corresponding FDA date. A Neo4j knowledge graph grounded in ATC-N, ICD-10, and MedDRA vocabularies preserves provenance, keeping every claim traceable and regulatory facts distinct from patient experience. These results establish source-aware integration as a route to more auditable psychiatric medication information, with usefulness and patient benefit to be tested prospectively.

</details>


### [271] [Accelerating Skill Assessment in Chess: A Drift-Diffusion-Enhanced Elo Rating System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26267)
*Tianyuan Zhou,Zhizheng Fu,Tianming Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DD-Elo 引入漂移扩散模型（DDM），通过整合逐手信息来改进 Elo 系统的响应延迟问题，在保持与传统 Elo 系统兼容的前提下，更准确、快速地评估棋手水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 Elo 等评分系统仅依赖胜负结果，导致对技能变化的响应存在滞后性；而直接利用逐手信息又面临状态空间庞大和数据噪声干扰的挑战。因此，需要一种既能利用细节信息又能克服噪声的更敏感的评估框架。

Method: 提出“漂移-扩散增强版 Elo 评分系统 (DD-Elo)”，引入认知神经科学中的漂移扩散模型 (DDM)，将棋手的水平表达建模为决策过程，并将棋局中的逐手信息处理噪声后融入评分计算。此外，文中通过数学推导证明了该系统与传统 Elo 系统相比偏差有限。

Result: 实验表明，DD-Elo 相比传统 Elo 系统能够更灵敏、更快速地捕捉棋手技能的波动，且保持了良好的理论一致性。

Conclusion: DD-Elo 提供了一种既具备可解释性、高响应速度，又向后兼容传统 Elo 系统的棋手水平评估方案，是衡量动态技能变化的有效工具。

Abstract: Rating systems such as Elo serve as the gold standard for matchmaking in competitive chess. However, they inherently suffer from response lag due to their exclusive reliance on match outcomes, neglecting the granular quality of gameplay. Nevertheless, incorporating move-by-move information into rating adjustments presents a significant challenge given the substantial noise and the vastness of the game-state space. To address this, we propose the Drift-Diffusion-Enhanced Elo Rating System (DD-Elo), a novel skill assessment framework inspired by the drift diffusion model (DDM) from cognitive neuroscience. By modeling skill expression as a decision-making process, our model integrates move-level data to capture rapid skill fluctuations. We provide a rigorous mathematical derivation proving that DD-Elo maintains a bounded deviation from the traditional Elo system, ensuring theoretical alignment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DD-Elo adapts to skill changes faster than Elo. Our findings suggest that DD-Elo offers an explainable, highly responsive, and backward-compatible solution for chess rating ecosystems. The implementation code is publicly available at https://github.com/Aquila-zhou1/DD-Elo .

</details>


### [272] [Governing Actions, Not Agents: Institutional Attestation as a Governance Model for Autonomous AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26298)
*Jakob Salfeld-Nebgen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对自主AI代理在高风险领域可能造成的不可逆后果，本文提出了一种基于“权威独立验证”的计算治理模型，通过限制代理的直接执行权（而非限制其推理能力），确保高风险操作的合规性与可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI代理开始执行临床处方或生产环境部署等具有后果性、不可逆的操作，传统的监控推理过程的方法已不足以应对安全风险。需要借鉴人类社会通过“第三方认证”控制高风险行为的治理逻辑。

Method: 提出了一种计算治理模型：AI代理保留自主规划权，但对高风险动作失去直接执行权。执行必须满足以下条件：由独立权威源验证前提条件、通过密码学绑定意图、由确定性策略评估，并将决策记录在防篡改日志中。

Result: 实现了概念证明（Proof-of-Concept），并通过软件部署和临床处方两个典型案例验证了该模型在限制高风险AI行为、提供可追溯性和可审计性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该模型通过将“执行权”与“规划/推理能力”解耦，提供了一种有效且实用的安全治理框架，在保持AI自主性的同时也确保了关键操作的可控性和可审计性。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents may begin to perform consequential, irreversible actions such as clinical prescribing and production software deployment. This paper observes that human institutions have governed powerful autonomous actors not by monitoring their reasoning but by requiring independently attested evidence at the point of consequential action. We formalise this institutional pattern as a computational governance model for AI agent systems. Under the proposed model, an agent retains full autonomy over planning and reasoning but holds no execution authority over designated high-risk actions. Execution is conditional on preconditions that are each independently attested by a separate authoritative source, cryptographically bound to a declared intent, and evaluated by a deterministic policy. Decisions are recorded in a tamper-evident log amenable to independent re-verification. We present a proof-of-concept implementation and illustrate the model with examples from software deployment and clinical prescribing.

</details>


### [273] [COrigami: An AI Pipeline for Co-Designing Flat-Foldable Visually Recognisable Origami](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26299)
*Tom Zahavy,Shaobo Hou,Thomas Tumiel,James Doran,Francesco Faccio,Xidong Feng,Alex Havrilla,Igor Khytryi,Chenglei Li,Lisa Schut,Vivek Veeriah,Arijan Abrashi,Michał Kosmulski,Robert J. Lang,Nick Robinson,Brandon Wong,Marcus Chiam,Gloria Fang,Satinder Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了COrigami，这是一个端到端的AI系统，旨在通过结合几何约束求解与强化学习引导的审美优化，将自然语言转化为数学上可行的折纸折痕图案，辅助人类艺术家进行创作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在解决可验证问题上表现出色，但在处理需要同时满足严格几何约束（如计算折纸的平面可折叠性方程式）和主观视觉美感的物理艺术创作时仍面临挑战。

Method: 提出了一套端到端的AI驱动流水线（COrigami），包含五个核心步骤：生成语义火柴人图、计算基础封装（base packing）、求解满足平面可折叠性的折痕图案、塑造折痕形状，以及通过基于自动审美评估循环的强化学习进行模型细化。

Result: COrigami系统能够从自然语言描述中生成结构化、数学上可行的折纸折痕图案，并有效地作为艺术家的协作助手，提供可进一步扩展和塑造的基础结构。

Conclusion: 该研究通过整合算法优化与自主审美评估，成功展示了AI系统如何满足多目标物理约束，从而实现数学严谨的“人机协作设计”，为计算折纸领域提供了一个高效的辅助创作工具。

Abstract: While generative AI has achieved remarkable success in solving problems with verifiable solutions, generating physical art that satisfies both strict geometric constraints and subjective visual aesthetics remains a challenge. This paper presents an approach to tackle these difficulties in the domain of computational origami, a mathematically rigid environment that grounds artistic design within the equations of flat foldability. We present COrigami, an end-to-end AI-driven pipeline that assists the design cycle by generating crease patterns from natural language. Our pipeline involves generating a semantic stick figure, computing a base packing, solving for a flat-foldable crease pattern, shaping the flat-folded crease pattern, and refining the generated model using reinforcement learning driven by an autonomous aesthetic evaluation loop. Our system acts as a highly effective collaborative assistant, generating structural starting points that human artists can further expand and shape. By integrating algorithmic optimisation with autonomous aesthetic critique, this work demonstrates how AI systems can satisfy multi-objective physical constraints to enable reliable, mathematically grounded co-creativity.

</details>


### [274] [The Verification Horizon: No Silver Bullet for Coding Agent Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26300)
*Binghai Wang,Chenlong Zhang,Dayiheng Liu,Jiajun Zhang,Jiawei Chen,Mouxiang Chen,Rongyao Fang,Siyuan Zhang,Xuwu Wang,Yuheng Jing,Zeyao Ma,Zeyu Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对当前编码智能体“生成易、验证难”的困境，本文提出了验证信号的质量维度框架，通过对比四种验证方案，指出验证器必须与生成模型协同进化，以解决奖励篡改问题并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在代码智能体领域，生成复杂候选解已不再是瓶颈，而“可靠地验证生成的解”成为了核心难题。由于意图模糊性（intent underspecified）和模型训练中的奖励篡改（reward hacking），现有的验证器难以忠实于人类意图。

Method: 1. 从可扩展性（scalability）、忠实度（faithfulness）和鲁棒性（robustness）三个维度构建验证信号质量分析框架；2. 对四种奖励构造方法（单元测试验证器、前端任务评分验证器、人工验证、自动化智能体验证）进行了深入实验与分析。

Result: 研究表明，有针对性的验证设计能有效抑制奖励篡改，提升任务完成质量；实验证明，在多个内部及公开基准测试中，优化后的验证策略显著提升了模型性能。

Conclusion: 验证机制不能是一成不变的，必须随着生成模型能力的提升而协同进化。单一的奖励函数无法长期保持有效，动态的、针对性的验证策略是提升智能体表现的关键。

Abstract: A classical intuition holds that verifying a solution is easier than producing one. For today's coding agents, this intuition is being inverted: as foundation models develop stronger reasoning capabilities and engineering harnesses grow more sophisticated, generating complex candidate solutions is no longer difficult -- reliably verifying them has become the harder problem. Every verifier we can build is only a proxy for human intent, never the intent itself. This makes verification subject to a twofold difficulty: first, intent is underspecified by nature, making it inherently hard to faithfully check whether it has been fulfilled; second, during model training, optimization widens the gap between proxy and intent -- manifesting as reward hacking or signal saturation. To address this, we characterize the quality of verification signals along three dimensions -- scalability, faithfulness, and robustness -- and argue that achieving all three simultaneously is the central challenge. We further study four reward constructions: a test verifier for general coding tasks, a rubric verifier for frontend tasks, the user as verifier for real-world agent tasks, and an automated agent verifier for long-horizon tasks. Across different task types and policy capability levels, we conduct in-depth analysis and experiments on the core challenges of reward design and how to more effectively leverage reward signals. Experiments show that targeted verification design can effectively suppress reward hacking, improve task completion quality, and achieve significant gains across multiple internal and public benchmarks. These experiences collectively point to a core observation: no fixed reward function can remain effective as policy capability continues to grow; and verification must co-evolve with the generator.

</details>


### [275] [How Do Tool-Augmented LLM Agents Perform on Real-World Energy Analytics Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26346)
*David Akinpelu,Akintonde Abbas,Rereloluwa Alimi,Ayodeji Lana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对能源领域缺乏动态任务评测的问题，研究提出了一个包含243个专家级问题的能源市场分析基准，通过集成实时API、专业工具和多维评估体系，实证检验了LLM代理在能源决策中的实际效能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用及特定领域代理基准测试繁多，但能源领域仍局限于静态知识回忆，缺乏针对实时数据获取、复杂法规环境、多步量化推理等真实世界能源市场任务的评估手段。

Method: 构建了一个包含243个专家策划问题的能源市场分析评测环境，涵盖数据检索分析、知识解读及高级量化建模。为代理配置了专门的工具套件（如实时电力市场API、法规搜索、关税数据库、资产优化模型），并通过多维评估协议（正确性、准确性、属性对齐、来源有效性）对比了开源与闭源大模型的表现。

Result: 该研究填补了能源领域动态任务评估的空白，通过实证分析揭示了当前大模型在对接专业工具、处理高风险能源决策任务时的效能，并公开了包含评估环境和工具套件在内的相关研究成果。

Conclusion: 大语言模型代理在能源分析领域既面临机遇也面临挑战，模型能力与领域专用工具的深度集成是解决复杂能源任务的关键缺口，该研究为未来能源领域AI代理的评测与开发提供了基础框架。

Abstract: Agentic benchmarks have emerged across general-purpose and domain-specific settings, including finance, coding, law, and drug discovery, yet energy-domain evaluations remain largely limited to static knowledge recall. This is a critical gap for a sector that requires live data retrieval, specialized regulatory and market knowledge, and multi-step quantitative reasoning under real-world constraints.
  We present an empirical study of tool-augmented LLM agents on real-world energy market analytics tasks. Our evaluation environment includes 243 expert-curated problems across three categories: (1) Market Data Retrieval and Analysis, (2) Knowledge Retrieval and Interpretation, and (3) Advanced Quantitative Modeling and Decision Analytics. Tasks include price and demand analysis, tariff impact modeling, asset revenue and returns estimation, hedging strategy analysis, and optimization modeling, with problems spanning multiple difficulty levels.
  Agents are equipped with a configurable suite of domain tools, including live electricity market APIs for major U.S. ISOs, regulatory docket search, utility tariff databases, asset optimization models, and retrieval-augmented generation over energy market documents. We assess agent responses using a multi-dimensional evaluation protocol that scores approach correctness, answer accuracy, attribute alignment, and source validity, with category-aware routing to match scoring criteria to question type. We evaluate both closed-source and open-source LLMs, providing a comparative analysis of how model capability and domain tooling interact in a high-stakes professional domain. Key artifacts are publicly released to support reproducibility and future research.

</details>


### [276] [Instruction Bleed: Cross-Module Interference in Prompt-Composed Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26356)
*Ching-Yu Lin,Yifan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文定义了“组合行为泄露”（CBL）现象，即Transformer注意力机制导致的模块间不可见的隐性行为干扰，并提供了一套评估此类风险的协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示组合式代理系统中存在一种难以察觉的失效模式：修改一个提示模块会隐性改变其他模块的行为，尽管它们之间没有共享变量或依赖关系，作者将其定义为组合行为泄露（CBL）。

Method: 提出了一个三通道协议（体积、内容、形式）来扰动非焦点模块；在Claude Sonnet 3.5（原文述及Claude Sonnet 4.6，应为笔误理解为同类模型）上进行了144次实验，旨在量化组合行为泄露（CBL）。

Result: 研究证实，内容通道的改变会导致显著的成对效应（Cohen's d = 0.63），虽然未导致推荐结果直接翻转，但这种亚阈值效应会在大规模决策中累积风险。

Conclusion: 组合行为泄露（CBL）是提示工程代理系统中的一种基本失效模式，源于Transformer架构的非隔离性。研究强调，在未来的代理评估中，必须将跨模块干扰测量纳入标准流程。

Abstract: Practitioners of prompt-composed agentic systems report a recurring failure mode: editing one prompt module silently shifts the behavior of others despite no shared variable or executable dependency. We formalize this as compositional behavioral leakage (CBL): interference between modules sharing a context window. CBL is enabled by architectural non-isolation: transformer self-attention provides no formal boundary between concatenated modules. We probe CBL on a deployed job-evaluation agent (Claude Sonnet 4.6, 144 trials) through a reusable three-channel protocol that perturbs non-focal modules along volume, content, and form. Only the content channel produces a detectable paired effect (Cohen's d = 0.63, bootstrap 95% CI excluding zero); no recommendation flipped -- a sub-threshold regime invisible to standard QA but compounding across the thousands of decisions a deployed agent makes. CBL is orthogonal to known agent-failure axes (adversarial injection, cognitive degradation, multi-agent fault propagation, privacy leakage). We contribute an operational definition, a reusable protocol, a falsifiable prediction set, and a system-class characterization, establishing cross-module interference measurement as a requirement for prompt-composed agent evaluation.

</details>


### [277] [What We are Missing in Multimodal LLM Evaluation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26348)
*Po-han Li,Shenghui Chen,Sandeep Chinchali,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 文章指出当前MLLM评估基准主要局限于孤立任务，忽略了跨模态深度整合能力。通过分析评估缺口，作者强调了在时空、物理、一致性和注意力维度构建新评估标准对于衡量多模态智能真实进展的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MLLM在多模态输入处理和文本生成方面进步迅速，但现有的评估方法大多局限于孤立任务，无法有效衡量模型跨模态整合信息的能力，导致难以准确识别模型的真实能力边界。

Method: 对现有的MLLM评估手段和基准测试体系（Benchmark Taxonomy）进行系统性综述与分析，并对比当前评估方案与多模态复杂任务要求之间的差异，从而识别现有的评估缺口（Gaps）。

Result: 文章识别出当前评估体系在四个关键维度存在缺失：时空一致性（temporal-spatial coherence）、物理世界理解（physical world understanding）、多模态一致性（multimodal consistency）以及选择性注意力（selective attention）。

Conclusion: 当前多模态大语言模型（MLLM）评估体系存在严重的碎片化和盲区，未来研究必须重点关注跨模态深度整合能力的评估（如时空一致性、物理世界理解等），以客观衡量多模态智能的真实进展。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can process diverse inputs, e.g., text, images, audio, and video, and generate textual responses. While their capabilities have advanced rapidly, evaluation of such models has not kept pace. Most existing evaluation benchmarks are limited to isolated tasks and reveal little about whether a model integrates information across modalities. We examine current means for evaluating MLLMs and review the existing benchmark taxonomy to identify gaps, including temporal-spatial coherence, physical world understanding, multimodal consistency, and selective attention. Addressing these gaps is essential for measuring real progress in multimodal intelligence and exposing capability boundaries.

</details>


### [278] [OpenFinGym: A Verifiable Multi-Task Gym Environment for Evaluating Quant Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26350)
*Kaicheng Zhang,Wen Ge,Lei Jiang,Weixin Yang,Jordan Langham-Lopez,Jialin Yu,Lukasz Szpruch,Hao Ni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文推出了 OpenFinGym，这是一个面向量化金融的统一评估框架，通过集成多阶段任务（从预测到交易）和严谨的执行接口，解决了现有研究中评估碎片化和缺乏现实金融相关性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量化金融智能体评估方法碎片化，往往忽视金融任务的关联性（多阶段工作流），导致高估了智能体在复杂、真实市场环境下的实际决策与泛化能力。

Method: 构建了一个集合预测、市场生成、实时交易和欺诈检测于一体的统一 Gym 环境；开发了自动化任务构建管道（将论文转化为可执行任务）、容器化运行环境（防泄漏）、低延迟纸面交易引擎以及支持监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）的集成接口。

Result: 提出并开源了 OpenFinGym 环境，提供了一套能够解决长周期预测、实时数据流处理及防数据泄露的综合性测试平台，为开发者构建通用金融智能体提供了工业级验证工具。

Conclusion: OpenFinGym 为量化金融智能体提供了一个统一、可验证且接近真实市场环境的评估框架，弥补了现有研究在任务孤立、缺乏金融相关性和验证机制方面的不足。

Abstract: Although large language model agents are increasingly applied to quantitative-finance workflows, their evaluation remains fragmented across isolated tasks, while the financial relevance of benchmark tasks is often overlooked. Yet financial workflows are inherently multi-stage, spanning interdependent tasks such as forecasting, strategy construction, risk management, and trading. Existing platforms typically focus on a single task, and can therefore overstate agent competence and fail to reveal weaknesses in generalization, real-market interaction, and financially meaningful decision-making. We introduce OpenFinGym, a unified gym environment for quantitative-finance agent development that covers forecasting, market generation, real-time trading, and fraud detection under a single execution and verification interface. OpenFinGym additionally provides an automated task-construction pipeline that turns quantitative finance publications into executable task packages; a containerised runtime with a host-side verifier service that supports scalable agent rollouts and prevents runtime train-test leakage; a paper trading engine with a low-latency data-stream design; deferred-resolution support for long-horizon and event-market forecasts; and integration for SFT and RL post-training

</details>


### [279] [Accelerating Returns and the Qualitative Engine for Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26359)
*Guojun Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 库兹韦尔的加速回报理论不足以解释科学发现的本质——核心难题是“定性推理”，而非简单的加速；本文提出“科学定性引擎”（QES）来弥补这一缺陷，强调保存科学智慧对人类的意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑“加速回报理论”是否足以解释科学发现的本质；指出当前 AI 在执行能力（计算力）上增长迅速，但在处理科学发现所需的“定性推理”上存在结构性缺陷。

Method: 通过数学化解读雷·库兹韦尔（Ray Kurzweil）的“加速回报理论”，并对比当前前沿 AI（如 ARC-AGI 测评）在定性推理上的局限性，论证定性推理在科学发现中的核心地位；提出“科学定性引擎”（QES）框架。

Result: 库兹韦尔的加速增长虽在基础设施和执行层面有效，但无法自动转化为解决问题的核心发现能力；当前 AI 系统在 ARC-AGI 上的低得分证明了其定性推理能力仍与人类水平存在巨大差距。

Conclusion: 加速回报理论不能解决科学发现的核心难题；科学发现依赖于定性推理，而非单纯的计算能力增长。作者提出“科学定性引擎”（QES）作为一种补充，强调保存和组织人类科学发现过程中的智慧，这对人类未来无论 AGI 何时到来都至关重要。

Abstract: Ray Kurzweil described a thesis of accelerating returns, which is the most influential narratives in discussions of technological progress. Its central claim is that advances in multiple technological fields, especially compute, artificial intelligence, brain science, and biotechnology, interact in such a way that progress becomes self-amplifying and approximately exponential. This paper gives a simple mathematical interpretation of that claim and then argues that, even if such acceleration is real, it does not by itself resolve the central problem of scientific discovery. The reason is that accelerating returns apply most naturally to executional and infrastructural capability, whereas genuine discovery often depends on a different capacity: qualitative reasoning about when a current framework is structurally inadequate and what conceptual move is needed next. Recent ARC-AGI-3 results sharpen this distinction: humans solve the benchmark at ceiling, whereas frontier AI systems remain below 1%, indicating that the gap between current AI and human flexible reasoning is still very large. At the same time, Demis Hassabis has emphasized that humans must retain their sense of meaning and what they choose to focus their lives on, a reminder that the future of AI is not only a technical forecast but also a question of what forms of human understanding are worth preserving and transmitting. This paper positions the Qualitative Engine for Science (QES) [3] as a response to that missing capacity. In this view, the Kurzweil theory helps explain why quantitative capability may accelerate, while QES addresses the central problem in scientific discovery that acceleration alone does not solve. Its value does not depend on when AGI arrives, but on the fact that the processes of scientific discovery themselves constitute a form of human wisdom worth preserving, organizing, and making accessible.

</details>


### [280] [Narration-of-Thought: Inference-Time Scaffolding for Defeasible Ethical Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26366)
*Patrick Cooper,Alvaro Velasquez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过引入NoT系统提示词结构，有效克服了大模型在道德决策中的关键认知偏差，实现了推理过程的可审计化和决策的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大模型思维链（CoT）在处理道德困境时存在两个关键失效模式：利益相关者遗漏（Stakeholder collapse）和不确定性抑制（Uncertainty suppression），导致决策过程缺乏透明度和可靠性。

Method: 引入“叙述思维”（Narration-of-Thought, NoT）系统提示词结构，将推理过程显式划分为五个阶段：主角、利益相关者、两步后果、不确定性、最终承诺。此外，结合了文本梯度下降优化和多轮多利益相关方辩论协议。

Result: 在100个DailyDilemmas场景测试中，NoT显著降低了各模型的失效模式发生率（利益相关者遗漏降至1%以下，不确定性抑制显着降低）；在复杂的多方辩论任务中，将一致达标率提升至95%-100%。验证了该方法不依赖额外训练或参数调整即可生效。

Conclusion: NoT框架通过结构化思维过程，有效解决了大模型在道德困境决策中的遗漏利益相关者和过度自信问题，为构建可靠、可审计的智能体提供了一种简单且高扩展性的方案。

Abstract: Standard chain-of-thought on moral dilemmas exhibits two failure modes: stakeholder collapse (the trace names at most one party with a stake in the outcome) and uncertainty suppression (no explicit unknowns or hedges before committing to an action). We introduce narration-of-thought (NoT), a system prompt that structures chain-of-thought into five sections: protagonist, stakeholders, two-step consequences, uncertainty, then commitment. NoT adds no training, parameters, or fine-tuning. On 100 DailyDilemmas scenarios across four generators from three vendors, NoT cuts stakeholder collapse from up to 31% to under 1% and uncertainty suppression from up to 72% to 1-24% on every model. A matched-budget verbose-CoT control rules out token spend as the active ingredient; NoT retains Cliff's delta advantages of +0.79 to +0.90 on stakeholder count and +0.65 to +0.93 on uncertainty score for three of four generators, and a section ablation attributes each shift to its specific sub-instruction. Textual-gradient descent initialised at NoT improves the scaffold further; a cross-family training judge (different vendor from the generator) dominates an in-family one on every measured axis. Extended to a five-round multi-stakeholder debate protocol, the scaffold converts a 6% standoff into 95% full consensus on a calibration set and 100% combined convergence on a DailyDilemmas replication. The resulting traces externalise the stakeholders, consequences, and uncertainty grounding each commitment, providing an auditable substrate for dependable agentic deployment.

</details>


### [281] [Geometry-Aware MCTS for Extremal Problems in Combinatorial Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26399)
*Luoning Zhang,Xu Zhuang,Tianhao Wang,Nathan Kaplan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对组合几何中的极端配置搜索问题，提出了一种几何感知 MCTS 框架，通过引入对称性利用与增量式约束强制，有效规避了计算瓶颈，并在多个经典问题上取得了新的最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决组合几何中点配置问题的计算复杂性；克服现有精确求解器遭受的组合爆炸，以及强化学习与 Transformer 模型在处理稀疏奖励和二次型令牌限制下的局限性。

Method: 提出了一种几何感知蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）框架，包含以下核心技术：1. 基于增量更新的约束强制执行机制（将复杂度从 O(n³) 降至 O(n²)）；2. 利用几何对称性进行候选节点修剪（缩减搜索分支）；3. 对称批处理转换以加速搜索。

Result: 在测试的六个问题中的五个刷新了最佳计算结果。例如，在 Max-N3IL 问题中发现了规模约为 1.8n 的配置（对于 82 ≤ n ≤ 119）；在最小完备集 (Smallest Complete Set) 问题中，发现了规模约为 0.95n 的配置，刷新了上限。

Conclusion: 提出了一种几何感知 MCTS 框架，通过强制执行限制和利用几何对称性，有效解决了组合几何中的极端问题，并在多个问题上刷新了计算标准。

Abstract: We study certain extremal problems in combinatorial geometry that ask about configurations of points in an $n \times n$ grid that satisfy strict, global geometric constraints. Classical exact solvers suffer from combinatorial explosion for these types of problems, and standard reinforcement learning and transformer-based models struggle with the sparse reward "validity cliff" and quadratic token-consumption limits. To overcome these bottlenecks, we propose a Geometry-Aware Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) framework. Our approach strictly enforces geometric constraints through incremental updates to the feasible action space. For constraints about collections of collinear points, like those that occur in the classic No-Three-in-Line problem (Max-N3IL), this mechanism reduces the constraint checking complexity from $O(n^3)$ to $O(n^2)$. To improve search efficiency, we exploit geometric symmetries in two ways: canonical pruning during node expansion to reduce the branching factor, and symmetric batch transitions to accelerate the discovery of promising configurations. We perform extensive experiments and establish new best-known computational results on five out of six of the problems that we considered. Notably, for Max-N3IL we find configurations of size roughly $1.8 n$ for grids of size $82 \le n \le 119$. For the Smallest Complete Set problem, we find configurations of size roughly $0.95 n$, providing new upper bounds within the tested grids. This work establishes Geometry-Aware MCTS as a highly adaptable framework for discovering novel configurations in combinatorial geometry.

</details>


### [282] [When Agents Meet Electric Bus Fleet Operations: Pricing Behavior, Trade-offs, and Policy Implications in an Aggregator Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26400)
*Jônatas Augusto Manzolli,Ali Eslami,Luis Miranda-Moreno,Jiangbo Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合优化算法与智能体（Agentic）的自动化调度框架，用于协调电动巴士运营与电网交互（V2G）；该系统能有效应对实时干扰并提升运营价值，但强调在公共交通场景下部署时，必须建立透明的监管与价值共享机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决电动巴士在运行中因服务可靠性、电池SOC、充电资源等多种复杂且动态的不确定性约束，导致调度管理困难的问题，并探索如何通过智能体系统实现车队与电网的高效协同。

Method: 构建了一个智能体聚合架构，其核心是“优化模型+监督智能体”的混合设计：优化模型确保调度方案的物理可行性（路线、充换电、V2G），智能体层负责实时干扰检测、电价适应性调整及调度方案评估，并具备触发重优化的决策能力。

Result: 实验表明，该架构能有效维持调度方案的可行性，通过智能选择重优化时机改善了充放电灵活性利用率；但同时也发现，若采用以利润为导向的定价策略，智能体聚合器会从公共交通运营商处获取不当利益，揭示了技术效率与公平性之间的冲突。

Conclusion: 在大规模电动巴士车队通过V2G参与电网调度时，智能体聚合架构（Agentic Aggregator）是有效的管理工具，但其实施必须配备透明的协调机制、可审计的费率设定及明确的价值分配规则，以防止利润导向的聚合器剥削公共交通运营商的利益。

Abstract: Agentic systems are changing how complex operational tasks are coordinated, introducing a new paradigm for connecting heterogeneous data sources and automating processes. Electric bus fleets provide a relevant test case. Their operation requires continuous coordination between service reliability, battery state-of-charge, charger availability, electricity prices, route-energy uncertainty, and vehicle-to-grid (V2G) opportunities. This paper proposes an agentic aggregator framework that streamlines this decision environment by coupling an optimization-based electric bus scheduling model with supervisory agents for disturbance detection, tariff adaptation, and schedule evaluation. The optimization core enforces physical feasibility across routes, chargers, batteries, and V2G exchanges, while the agentic layer interprets changing operating conditions, triggers real-time re-optimization when needed, and defines how flexibility value is allocated between the aggregator and the public transport operator (PTO). A realistic depot case study evaluates day-ahead and real-time operations under profit-based and operation-based coordination modes, considering service delays, route-energy deviations, electricity price shocks, and combined disturbances. The results show that agentic aggregation can support adaptive fleet-grid coordination by maintaining feasible schedules, activating re-optimization selectively, and improving the use of charging and V2G flexibility. However, they also reveal a critical trade-off: the same agentic capability that reduces operational complexity can extract value from the PTO when configured around profit-oriented pricing. These findings suggest that agentic aggregators can become useful for managing electric bus V2G operations, but their deployment in public-fleet contexts requires transparent coordination modes, auditable tariff-setting, and explicit value-sharing rules.

</details>


### [283] [Unbiased Canonical Set-Valued Oracles Via Lattice Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26418)
*Jobst Heitzig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的AI预测机制，通过构造信度集（而非单一概率值）来解决预测被采纳后导致预测失效的自洽性问题，并利用不动点定理保证了模型解的规范性与一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非代理型Oracle AI在处理执行性预测任务时面临自我指涉问题：AI的预测一经发布并被采取行动，就会改变其预测对象的概率。现有的“反事实”方案会导致预测结果失效，因此需要一种能反馈并包含自身影响的自洽预测模型。

Method: 利用格论（Lattice-theoretic）方法，特别是Knaster–Tarski不动点定理，在闭信度集的完备格上定义单调算子，通过求取最小不动点来确定规范的预测区间，并证明了该构造的存在性、自洽性和非空性。

Result: 证明了该方法在非执行性问题中可退化为经典点估计值；对于二元事件，该 canonical answer 表现为一个包含所有自洽点估计值的区间；该方法已成功从二元事件推广至任意随机变量。

Conclusion: 该研究成功提出了一种基于Knaster–Tarski不动点定理的非代理型Oracle AI预测框架，能够生成非平凡且具备自洽性的信度集（Credal Sets），在处理执行性预测（performative prediction）时优于传统的反事实方案。

Abstract: A non-agentic "oracle" AI that estimates probabilities of future events faces a self-reference problem: once its answer is learned and acted upon, it can change the very probability it was asked to report. One response, advocated for the Scientist AI programme, is to ask only counterfactual questions, evaluated as if the answer had no influence. We observe that such answers tend to become irrelevant the moment they are learned, precisely because their premise is then false. We therefore explore a self-referential alternative in which the oracle reports not a single probability but a credal set that is simultaneously unbiased and self-consistent with the consequences of being learned. The naive self-consistency requirement is satisfied by too many sets (including the useless answer $[0,1]$), so the problem is to single out a canonical, nontrivial member. We do so with the Knaster--Tarski fixed-point theorem on the complete lattice of closed credal sets, taking the least fixed point of a suitably defined isotone operator; a variant instead reports the least fixed point that contains every self-consistent point estimate. We prove existence, self-consistency, and nonemptiness, show that the construction collapses to the classical point answer for non-performative questions, and that for a binary event the canonical answer is, under a natural hull-factoring assumption, an interval. The development is purely lattice-theoretic and extends unchanged from a binary event $B$ to an arbitrary random variable $X$, with $P(B\mid A,C)$ replaced by the conditional law $\mathcal{L}(X\mid A,C)$. We close with open questions, including whether the interval characterization itself survives that generalization.

</details>


### [284] [AgentX: Towards Agent-Driven Self-Iteration of Industrial Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26859)
*Changxin Lao,Fei Pan,Guozhuang Ma,Han Li,Huihuang Lin,Jijun Shi,Kangzhi Zhao,Kun Gai,Mo Zhou,Qinqin Zhou,Quan Chen,Ruochen Yang,Shifu Bie,Shuang Yang,Shuo Yang,Wenhao Li,Wentao Xie,Xiao Lv,Xuming Wang,Yijun Wang,Yiming Chen,Yusheng Huang,Zhongyuan Wang,Zibo Zhao,Zijie Zhuang,Baoning Xia,Chao Liu,Chaoyi Ma,Chubo He,Dawei Cong,Feng Jiang,Gang Wang,Guilin Xia,Hanwen Xu,Jiahong Xie,Jiahui Qiao,Jian Liang,Jiangfan Yue,Jing Wang,Jinghan Yang,Jinghui Jia,Kan Qin,Lei Wang,Ming Li,Peilin Song,Pengbo Xu,Qiang Luo,Ruiming Tang,Shiyang Liu,Shuxian Jin,Tao Wang,Tao Zhang,Xiang Gao,Xianghan Li,Yingsong Luo,Yiwen Ning,Yongcheng Liu,Yuan Guo,Zhaojie Liu,Zhenkai Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentX是一个端到端的自主多智能体系统，通过闭环自动化实现推荐算法实验的持续生成、部署、评估与自我优化，突破了传统人力驱动的研发瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前推荐系统研发高度依赖人工，导致研发周期长、创新难以随计算资源规模化，存在严重的结构性执行瓶颈。

Method: 提出AgentX系统，包含四个紧密耦合的智能体阶段：1. Brainstorm Agent（生成实验建议）；2. Developing Agent（自动化代码开发与验证）；3. Evaluation Agent（安全在线实验A/B测试与闭环评估）；4. Harness Evolution layer (SGPO)（基于语义梯度下降的系统自进化机制）。

Result: AgentX实现了推荐系统实验的自主闭环，能够在无需人工介入的情况下，完成从创意生成到代码部署、实验评估及后续策略优化的全流程，实现了研发过程的规模化与自我优化。

Conclusion: AgentX通过实现从人工驱动到自主闭环的范式转变，有效解决了推荐系统研发过程中的人力瓶颈，证明了多智能体系统在复杂工业推荐场景下的可扩展性和自我进化潜力。
